~~~python
    show('RELAX = 1/10', check_history(S, G10, HIST, rho=F(1, 10))[0])
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    sat = ([('AB', (F(2), F(2)))] * 6 + [('A', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('B', (F(2), 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6) * 2
    show('饱和史（A_PLUS = A_MINUS = 1/2）', check_history(S, Gs, sat)[0])
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 8), F(1, 16)
    rng = random.Random(1101)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    agg = dict(ticks=0, events=0, clip=0, closure_bad=0, gen_bad=0, d4_bad=0, d13_bad=0, edges=0, d7_bad=0)
    for _ in range(100):
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)])
        Gr = GC.snn_gate(S, rho)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(50)]
        w0 = [[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        o0 = (w0, [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        stt_r, _ = check_history(S, Gr, hist, o0=o0, plastic=rng.random() < .9, bap=rng.random() < .9, rho=rho)
        for k in agg: agg[k] += stt_r[k]
    show('随机史 100 条（种子 1101）', agg)
    print(f'  RPath（s1）：X = 8 位，T = {T}，见证步 {len(rpath["steps"])} 个；Trace、Ident 为证据值，由 gate_criteria.py 求出')
    print('三、读取集充分性（引理 R2；扰动读取集之外的全部结点为随机值；种子 7；含夹限事件）')
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
    Gsat = GC.snn_gate(S)
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 8), F(1, 16)
    trials, ctr, viol = readset_check([G, G10, Gsat])
    print(f'  {trials} 次事件—扰动对（其中夹限事件 {ctr} 次），违例 {viol}')
    print('四、不干涉引理（引理 R3）：槽位依赖图前向可达集之外，任意赋值干预不改变结点值与事件（种子 11）')
    ni = noninterference_check([G, G10, Gsat])
    print(f'  {ni["runs"]} 次随机史与随机源集；结点比较 {ni["node_checks"]}，事件比较 {ni["ev_checks"]}，违例 {ni["viol"]}')
    bad = sum(s_['closure_bad'] + s_['gen_bad'] + s_['d4_bad'] + s_['d13_bad'] + s_['d7_bad'] for s_ in tot)
    print('五、结论（有限核验）：', '全部史上 K_t 逐拍一致、两路见证相同、D4、D7、D13 无违例；读取集充分性与不干涉均无违例。'
          if bad == 0 and viol == 0 and ni['viol'] == 0 else f'存在违例 {bad} / {viol} / {ni["viol"]}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.4　gate\_criteria.py　申报实例、合法设定运行、第三篇判词路径的求值库（3.6、7.5）

~~~python
"""门控类的基本求值库：申报实例 Instance（形成记录、可用位）、合法设定运行、实际事件图与槽位可达、第三篇判词路径与 F 编码读数的求值器（正文 3.6、7.5 的对照用）。
库模块，不单独运行。只用标准库，精确有理运算。
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
EPS = F(1, 100)
TPOS = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]
NONE = ('none', (0, 0))
SAT, COV, PROF, UNDEF, RDEF = '⊥_Sat', '⊥_Cov', '⊥_Prof', '⊥_Undef', '⊥_Rdef'

def fr(q):
    return f'{q}' if q.denominator < 10 ** 6 else f'{float(q):.6f}'

def k_and(*vals):
    if 0 in vals: return 0
    if any(isinstance(v, str) for v in vals): return '⊥'
    return 1

def k_or(vals):
    vals = list(vals)
    if 1 in vals: return 1
    if any(isinstance(v, str) for v in vals): return '⊥'
    return 0

# ---------------- 运行与干预 ----------------
def run_with(G, o0, hist, t_start=0, interv=None, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """自拍 t_start 起按 hist 运行；interv(t, o) 在每拍执行前返回（可能被设定的）拍前状态。"""
    pre, evs, gates = {}, {}, {}
    o = o0
    for n, sig in enumerate(hist):
        t = t_start + n
        if interv is not None:
            o = interv(t, o)
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t, plastic, bap)
        o2, rec = G.step(o, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        pre[t], evs[t], gates[t] = o, e, rec['g']
        o = o2
    pre[t_start + len(hist)] = o
    return pre, evs, gates

def setw(o, assign):
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]
    for (_, i, j), val in assign.items():
        w[i][j] = val
    return (w, o[1], o[2], o[3])

def zk(G, o, k):
    return o[0][k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]

class Instance:
    """申报实例：G、史、窗 W = [t0, len(hist)−1]、T、协议开关。入口拍 t0 − 1 在史内。"""
    def __init__(self, name, G, hist, t0, T=TPOS, plastic=True, bap=True, o0=None):
        self.name, self.G, self.hist, self.t0, self.T = name, G, hist, t0, T
        self.t1 = len(hist) - 1
        self.kw = dict(plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        self.o0 = o0 or G.init()
        self.pre, self.evs, self.gates = run_with(G, self.o0, hist, **self.kw)
        self.form = {k: [] for k in T}     # D16（逐分项越阈）；R 层沿用
        for t in range(len(hist)):
            for e in self.evs[t].values():
                k = e['tgt']
                if k in T and abs(e['c']) > EPS and zk(G, self.pre[t], k) <= EPS < zk(G, self.pre[t], k) + e['c']:
                    self.form[k].append(t)
        self.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(hist) + 1)
                          if zk(G, self.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in self.form[k])] for k in T}
        self._cf = None
        self._R = None
    def is_avail(self, k, t):
        return t in self.avail[k]
    def Wevents(self):
        return [e for t in range(self.t0, self.t1 + 1) for e in self.evs[t].values()]
    # ---- 合法设定运行（正判候选）与包络钉位（负判） ----
    def cf(self):
        if self._cf is not None:
            return self._cf
        G, T = self.G, self.T
        runs = []
        av_any = [t for k in T for t in self.avail[k] if t <= self.t1]
        if not av_any:
            self._cf = (runs, {}, None)
            return self._cf
        first = min(av_any)
        for u in range(first, self.t1 + 1):
            av = [k for k in T if self.is_avail(k, u)]
            if not av:
                continue
            for ks in [[k] for k in av] + ([av] if len(av) > 1 else []):
                for lab in ('b', 'hi'):
                    assign = {k: {'b': G.b, 'hi': G.hi}[lab][k[1]][k[2]] for k in ks}
                    if all(assign[k] == self.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]] for k in ks):
                        continue
                    pre, evs, gates = run_with(G, setw(self.pre[u], assign), self.hist[u:], t_start=u, **self.kw)
                    runs.append(dict(u=u, ks=ks, lab=lab, evs=evs, gates=gates))
        pins = {}
        for lab in ('hi', 'lo'):
            def interv(t, o, lab=lab):
                a = {}
                for k in T:
                    if self.is_avail(k, t):
                        if lab == 'hi':
                            a[k] = G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]
                        elif o[0][k[1]][k[2]] > G.b[k[1]][k[2]]:
                            a[k] = G.b[k[1]][k[2]]
                return setw(o, a) if a else o
            pre, evs, gates = run_with(G, self.pre[first], self.hist[first:], t_start=first, interv=interv, **self.kw)
            flip = [t for t in gates if gates[t] != self.gates[t]]
            pins[lab] = dict(evs=evs, gates=gates, first_flip=min(flip) if flip else None)
            runs.append(dict(u=first, ks=list(T), lab='钉位' + lab, evs=evs, gates=gates))
        self._cf = (runs, pins, first)
        return self._cf
    def reach(self):
        if self._R is None:
            srcs = {(k, u) for k in self.T for u in self.avail[k] if u <= self.t1}
            self._R = RW.reach_forward(self.G, self.hist, srcs, **self.kw)
        return self._R

def same_event(evsB, e):
    t, eid = e['t'], e['id']
    if t not in evsB:
        return True
    if eid not in evsB[t]:
        return False
    return evsB[t][eid]['c'] == e['c']

def covered(I, e, first=None):
    """引理 C 覆盖的事件种类：其发生与分项只依门控序列、入口字母、x、y 与 T 外结构位。
    引理 C′（扩展）：门控序列不变时，v_j(t) 只经「上次复位之后、t 之前」各拍对 T 中指向 j 之位的读取而依 T；
    该区间（并截于首个可设拍之后）内无此种读取时，LEAK_j@t 覆盖；RESET_j@t 另要求拍 t 本身也无此种读取。"""
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    Tset = set(I.T)
    tj = {k[2] for k in I.T}
    if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'YIN', 'YDEC', 'XDEC', 'XIN', 'INJ'):
        return True
    if kind in ('SYN', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
        return ('w', idx[0], idx[1]) not in Tset
    if kind in ('LEAK', 'RESET'):
        j = idx[0]
        if j not in tj:
            return True
        if first is None or not hasattr(I, 'gates'):
            return False
        t = e['t']
        last = max([u for u in range(t) if I.gates[u][j]], default=-1)
        lo_t = max(last, first - 1)
        hi_t = t if kind == 'RESET' else t - 1
        for u in range(lo_t + 1, hi_t + 1):
            s = I.G.pat[I.hist[u][0]]
            if any(s[k[1]] for k in I.T if k[2] == j):
                return False
        return True
    return False

def dep(I, e):
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    t = e['t']
    for r in sorted(runs, key=lambda r: -r['u']):
        if r['u'] <= t and not same_event(r['evs'], e):
            return 1, f"LOC 拍{r['u']} {','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}' for k in r['ks'])}←{r['lab']}"
    if not pins:
        return 0, '无可用位'
    if covered(I, e, first) and all(pins[l]['first_flip'] is None or pins[l]['first_flip'] > t for l in ('hi', 'lo')):
        return 0, '引理 C（两钉位至该拍无门控翻转）'
    return COV, '钉位翻转或种类不在引理 C 覆盖内，所试设定未改变该事件'

def path(I, e):
    R = I.reach()
    return 1 if (e['reads'] & R[e['t']]) else 0

def eta(I, e):
    """η（或 κ）的判值与依据。返回 (值, 依据, Dep 值, Path 值, 超定标记)。"""
    p = path(I, e)
    if p == 0:
        d = (0, '无途径')
        return 0, '无途径（引理 R3）', 0, 0, False
    dv, why = dep(I, e)
    if dv == 1:
        return 1, why, 1, 1, False
    if dv == 0:
        return 0, '超定：有途径而无依赖（' + why + '）', 0, 1, True
    return COV, why, dv, 1, False

def ko_act(G, o, evs, eid, eps=EPS):
    """引理 A：KO 认证的 a。返回 (a, KO 差)。"""
    e = evs[eid]; k = e['tgt']
    if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP':
        return None, None
    if k[0] != 'w':
        d = e['c']
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    i, j = k[1], k[2]
    terms = [x['c'] for x in evs.values() if x['tgt'] == k and x['id'][0] != 'CLIP']
    S = o[0][i][j] + sum(terms)
    S_ko = S - e['c']
    clip = lambda z: min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], z))
    inside = lambda z: G.lo[i][j] <= z <= G.hi[i][j]
    d = clip(S) - clip(S_ko)
    if inside(S) and inside(S_ko):
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    return (1 if abs(d) > eps else SAT), d

def evaluate(I, part_all=False):
    """逐项求值本篇判据。part_all=True 时 Part 逐一求出全部 B 内事件的 κ（用于统计），否则遇见证即止。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    rows, hd, hd_cons = [], [], []
    n_over = 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['tgt'] not in T:
            continue
        a, d = ko_act(G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])
        if a is None:
            a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0   # 夹限事件不作敲除对象；其作用由分项判（分项判据，一般式 105 行）
        ev, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        n_over += over and a == 1
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, d=d, eta=ev, why=why, dep=dv, path=pv, over=over))
        hd.append(k_and(a, ev))
        hd_cons.append(k_and(a, '⊥_Over' if over else ev))
    part, part_cons, wit, nk = [], [], None, 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['car'] != RW.B:
            continue
        kv, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        nk += 1
        part.append(kv); part_cons.append('⊥_Over' if over else kv)
        if kv == 1 and wit is None:
            wit = (e['id'], why)
            if not part_all:
                break
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    q = {k: (int(zk(G, end, k) > EPS), int(bool(I.form[k])), zk(G, end, k)) for k in T}
    qv = k_and(*[k_and(r, f) for (r, f, _) in q.values()])
    trace = k_and(RDEF, qv, 1, 1)      # 本求值器不含 r 的 R 层规则（r 由附录 A.14 求）；Ident（POS）、HistEv（REC）由模型结构为 1
    out = dict(rows=rows, HDyn=k_or(hd), HDyn_cons=k_or(hd_cons), Part=k_or(part), Part_cons=k_or(part_cons),
               Part_wit=wit, n_over=n_over, q=q, qv=qv, Trace=trace)
    out['Q'] = k_and(trace, out['Part'], out['HDyn'])
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    out['pins'] = {l: pins[l]['first_flip'] for l in pins} if pins else {}
    out['nruns'] = len(runs)
    out['Wmax_z'] = {k: max(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    out['Wmin_z'] = {k: min(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    return out

# ---------------- 第三篇在本实例上的判词路径 ----------------
def third_path(I, nonF):
    """按《沉积场判定式》v1.0 条文逐级求第三篇可给的判词。nonF：nonf_encoding 对本宿载的结论（True 表示无合 BEAT、LOC 的精确 F 编码）。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    allpos = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    steps = []
    steps.append('Y₂：SEP 要求「宿载属 F 类」（〔第三篇〕184）；机制级申报入口要求「律属 F 类」（181）。'
                 + ('本宿载无合 BEAT、LOC 的精确 F 编码（命题 N1、N2），SEP 不能如实申报，Y₂ 判词不取得；同实例乙「反馈律不属 F 类三种数值律，定理六不适用（U9）。故只能用 Y₁」（639）。'
                    if nonF else 'F 编码存在，Y₂ 可用（本实例不出现此支）。'))
    steps.append('若强行以 488 型编码（发生按实际申报、常数律）通过入口：EF4 不成立（485），定理七（二）负判不成立；LOC 响应与编码的空支持冲突，属 E25（554）。其 F 层读数见 F 编码栏。')
    q = {}
    for k in T:
        q[k] = 0 if zk(G, end, k) <= EPS else PROF
    steps.append('Y₁（4.5，241）：q(x) 窗末 s(x) ≤ ε 判 0，否则 ⊥_Prof → ' + str({f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}': q[k] for k in T}))
    steps.append('Y₁（242）：r 判 ⊥_Prof（定理五（二））')
    low = all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in T)
    ph = 0 if low else PROF
    steps.append(f'Y₁（243）：Part、HDyn 在「W 内 T 各位恒不大于 ε」时判 0，否则 ⊥_Prof → W 内 T 恒 ≤ ε：{low} ⇒ Part = HDyn = {ph}')
    steps.append('Y₁（244）：Act(m)、HistCond(m) 无清单判 ⊥_Undef；（245）Fld 判 ⊥_Prof')
    fld_neg = all(zk(G, end, k) <= EPS for k in allpos) or all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in allpos)
    steps.append('Y₁ 加申报（247）：q = 1 须「F 类申报与 REC」——F 类申报不可得，q 仍 ⊥_Prof；'
                 f'（248）Fld 退化负判须 Dom5 且窗末无位 > ε 或 W 内一切位恒 ≤ ε：{fld_neg}；'
                 '（249）r 须 PE（196）——信号经教学电流门控界内可塑性，PE 不成立；'
                 '（250）Part 须 AC（198）——输出发放不是纯读取过程（发放写 y 并门控 PLUS），AC 不成立；'
                 '（251）HDyn 须 SAM 与 LOC 且 F 类（引理十，494）——F 类不成立')
    fld = 0 if fld_neg else PROF
    return dict(q=q, r=PROF, Part=ph, HDyn=ph, Fld=fld, steps=steps)

# ---------------- 488 型 F 编码的读数（D10—D19） ----------------
def f_layer(I, positions, ext_entry, eps=EPS):
    """入口拍 t0−1 以申报事件给出 z(t0)；W 内每一作用于位集的可塑性事件按实际时刻编码为常数律机制（发生已申报，cert_eff = 1）。
    ext_entry=True 时 T 位的入口事件记为界外脉冲（E2），否则为 B 内常数律机制（E1）。按 D12、D15—D19、4.2 求值。"""
    G, T, t0, t1 = I.G, I.T, I.t0, I.t1
    s = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
    form, mark = {k: [] for k in positions}, {k: [] for k in positions}
    dom_err = None
    for k in positions:
        z0 = zk(G, I.pre[t0], k)
        if z0 == 0:
            continue
        if z0 < 0:
            dom_err = dom_err or f'入口拍位 w{k[1]}{k[2]} 的 z(t0) = {fr(z0)} < 0，越出 F 定义域（D10、D20）'
            continue
        if ext_entry and k in T:
            if z0 > eps: form[k].append(t0 - 1); mark[k].append(t0 - 1)
        elif z0 > eps:
            form[k].append(t0 - 1)
        s[k] = z0
    devs, atoms = [], []
    for t in range(t0, t1 + 1):
        incr = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] in positions and e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
                c = e['c']
                act = 1 if abs(c) > eps else 0          # D18 Act_F
                hc = 0                                  # 常数律 Supp_act = ∅（D15）⇒ HistCond_F = 0（D18 后说明）
                atoms.append(dict(id=e['id'], t=t, tgt=e['tgt'], c=c, Act=act, HistCond=hc, Cond=0))
                if act:
                    if s[e['tgt']] <= eps < s[e['tgt']] + c:
                        form[e['tgt']].append(t)
                    incr[e['tgt']] += c                 # 4.2：只入账有效分项
        for k in positions:
            s[k] += incr[k]
            if s[k] < 0 and dom_err is None:
                dom_err = f'拍 {t} 位 w{k[1]}{k[2]} 更新值 {fr(s[k])} < 0（D20）'
        for k in positions:
            if s[k] != zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k):
                devs.append((t, k, s[k] - zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k)))
    Src = 1 if any(mark[k] for k in T) else 0
    Ret = 1 if all(s[k] > eps and form[k] for k in T) else 0
    trace = k_and(Src, Ret, 1, 1)
    part = k_or([a['Cond'] for a in atoms])
    hdyn = k_or([k_and(1 if a['tgt'] in T else 0, a['Act'], a['HistCond']) for a in atoms])
    return dict(Src=Src, Ret=Ret, Trace=trace, Part=part, HDyn=hdyn, Q=k_and(trace, part, hdyn),
                atoms=atoms, devs=devs, dom_err=dom_err)
~~~

A.5　lp\_exact.py　精确有理线性可行性与证书（5.1）

~~~python
"""精确有理线性可行性（Fourier–Motzkin，含严格不等式）：可行时给见证点，不可行时给 Motzkin 证书；正文 5.1 定理 L1、引理 L2、L3 的有限自检。
只用标准库。运行：python3 lp_exact.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True

def _norm(c):
    return {k: v for k, v in c.items() if v != 0}

def check_point(cons, x):
    for coef, const, strict in cons:
        val = sum(v * x.get(k, F(0)) for k, v in coef.items()) + const
        if (strict and not val < 0) or (not strict and not val <= 0):
            return False
    return True

def check_cert(cons, lam):
    if any(l < 0 for l in lam.values()):
        return False
    tot = {}
    s = F(0)
    strict_pos = False
    for i, l in lam.items():
        if l == 0:
            continue
        coef, const, strict = cons[i]
        for k, v in coef.items():
            tot[k] = tot.get(k, F(0)) + l * v
        s += l * const
        strict_pos = strict_pos or strict
    if any(v != 0 for v in tot.values()):
        return False
    return s > 0 or (s == 0 and strict_pos)

def feasible(cons):
    """Fourier–Motzkin。每条导出约束携带其关于原约束的非负乘子。"""
    rows = []
    for i, (coef, const, strict) in enumerate(cons):
        rows.append((_norm(dict(coef)), F(const), bool(strict), {i: F(1)}))
    order = []
    for r in rows:
        for k in r[0]:
            if k not in order:
                order.append(k)
    stages = []                  # 每次消元前的行集，用于回代
    for var in order:
        stages.append((var, rows))
        pos, neg, zero = [], [], []
        for r in rows:
            a = r[0].get(var, F(0))
            (pos if a > 0 else neg if a < 0 else zero).append(r)
        new = list(zero)
        seen = set()
        for rp in pos:
            ap = rp[0][var]
            for rn in neg:
                an = -rn[0][var]
                # an·rp + ap·rn 消去 var
                coef = {}
                for k, v in rp[0].items():
                    coef[k] = coef.get(k, F(0)) + an * v
                for k, v in rn[0].items():
                    coef[k] = coef.get(k, F(0)) + ap * v
                coef = _norm(coef)
                coef.pop(var, None)
                const = an * rp[1] + ap * rn[1]
                strict = rp[2] or rn[2]
                lam = {}
                for i, l in rp[3].items():
                    lam[i] = lam.get(i, F(0)) + an * l
                for i, l in rn[3].items():
                    lam[i] = lam.get(i, F(0)) + ap * l
                key = (tuple(sorted(coef.items())), const, strict)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                new.append((coef, const, strict, lam))
        rows = new
        for r in rows:          # 早停：出现常数矛盾
            if not r[0] and (r[1] > 0 or (r[1] == 0 and r[2])):
                return False, r[3]
    for r in rows:
        if r[1] > 0 or (r[1] == 0 and r[2]):
            return False, r[3]
    # 回代求见证点
    x = {}
    for var, rws in reversed(stages):
        lo, lo_s, hi, hi_s = None, False, None, False
        for coef, const, strict, _ in rws:
            a = coef.get(var, F(0))
            if a == 0:
                continue
            rest = sum(v * x[k] for k, v in coef.items() if k != var) + const
            bnd = -rest / a          # a·var + rest (<,≤) 0
            if a > 0:                # var (<,≤) bnd
                if hi is None or bnd < hi or (bnd == hi and strict):
                    hi, hi_s = bnd, strict if (hi is None or bnd < hi) else (hi_s or strict)
            else:                    # var (>,≥) bnd
                if lo is None or bnd > lo or (bnd == lo and strict):
                    lo, lo_s = bnd, strict if (lo is None or bnd > lo) else (lo_s or strict)
        if lo is None and hi is None:
            val = F(0)
        elif lo is None:
            val = hi - 1 if hi_s else hi
        elif hi is None:
            val = lo + 1 if lo_s else lo
        else:
            val = (lo + hi) / 2 if (lo_s or hi_s) else lo
        x[var] = val
    return True, x

def selftest(n=4000, seed=20261102):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    st = dict(feas=0, infeas=0, bad=0)
    for _ in range(n):
        d = rng.randint(1, 4)
        vs = [f'x{i}' for i in range(d)]
        m = rng.randint(1, 9)
        cons = []
        for _m in range(m):
            coef = {v: F(rng.randint(-4, 4), rng.randint(1, 3)) for v in vs if rng.random() < 0.8}
            cons.append((coef, F(rng.randint(-6, 6), rng.randint(1, 4)), rng.random() < 0.5))
        ok, obj = feasible(cons)
        if ok:
            st['feas'] += 1
            st['bad'] += not check_point(cons, obj)
        else:
            st['infeas'] += 1
            st['bad'] += not check_cert(cons, obj)
    # 边界例：x ≤ 0 且 x ≥ 0 可行；x < 0 且 x ≥ 0 不可行；x < 1 且 x > 0 可行（取中点）
    edge = [([({'x': F(1)}, F(0), False), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), False)], True),
            ([({'x': F(1)}, F(0), True), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), False)], False),
            ([({'x': F(1)}, F(-1), True), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), True)], True),
            ([({}, F(0), True)], False), ([({}, F(0), False)], True)]
    for cons, exp in edge:
        ok, obj = feasible(cons)
        st['bad'] += (ok != exp) or (ok and not check_point(cons, obj)) or ((not ok) and not check_cert(cons, obj))
    return st

if __name__ == '__main__':
    st = selftest()
    print('精确有理 Fourier–Motzkin 自检（随机 4000 组，维数 1—4，严格与非严格混合；另 5 个边界例）')
    print(f'  可行 {st["feas"]}（见证点逐条代入核验），不可行 {st["infeas"]}（Motzkin 证书独立核验），核验失败 {st["bad"]}')
~~~

A.6　decision.py　定理 D1、D2 的判定器，T\_max 覆盖（5.3、5.5）

~~~python
"""门控类 R 层判定器（正文 5.2、5.3 与第六节）：𝒟₁ 上函数依赖的精确判定（定理 D1）、Part 与 HDyn 判词与三档负向（定理 D2）、T_max 覆盖（定理 TM）。
声明的干预域：𝒟₁（单拍联合 LOC，σ_k ∈ [b_k, hi_k]）；𝒟_m（多拍）只有引理 C、C′ 的充分负判。
夹限个别化：饱和的位—拍按「分列」与「联合（D9）」两读法各求，一致则取之，否则 ⊥_Ind。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 decision.py（输出含「用时」行，随机器变化，其余确定）
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import lp_exact as LP
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr
# ---------- 仿射式：(常数, {变量: 系数}) ----------

def A0(c):
    return (F(c), {})

def avar(name):
    return (F(0), {name: F(1)})

def aadd(*xs):
    c, d = F(0), {}
    for x0, x1 in xs:
        c += x0
        for k, v in x1.items():
            d[k] = d.get(k, F(0)) + v
    return (c, {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v != 0})

def ascale(a, s):
    return (a[0] * s, {k: v * s for k, v in a[1].items() if v * s != 0})

def is_const(a):
    return not a[1]

def le0(a):      # a ≤ 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], False)

def lt0(a):      # a < 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], True)

class Stats:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lp = 0
        self.nodes = 0

ST = Stats()

def feas(R):
    ST.lp += 1
    return LP.feasible(R)

def simplify(R):
    """同方向约束只留最紧者（系数向量按最大绝对值归一）。等价变形，不改变可行集（正文引理 L3）。"""
    best = {}
    for coef, const, strict in R:
        m = max(abs(v) for v in coef.values())
        key = tuple(sorted((k, v / m) for k, v in coef.items()))
        c = const / m
        old = best.get(key)
        if old is None or c > old[0] or (c == old[0] and strict and not old[1]):
            best[key] = (c, strict)
    return [(dict(k), c, s_) for k, (c, s_) in best.items()]

def split(R, conds):
    """conds：[(标签, [约束])]；返回可行的 (标签, R + 约束)。常值约束直接求值。"""
    out = []
    for lab, cs in conds:
        add, ok = [], True
        for c in cs:
            if not c[0]:
                v = c[1]
                if (c[2] and not v < 0) or (not c[2] and not v <= 0):
                    ok = False
                    break
            else:
                add.append(c)
        if not ok:
            continue
        R2 = simplify(R + add) if add else R
        if add:
            f, _ = feas(R2)
            if not f:
                continue
        out.append((lab, R2))
    return out

def sym_step(G, st, R, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """定义 2 的符号一拍。st = (w{(i,j): 仿射}, v[仿射], x[常], y[常])。返回 [(R′, st′, 事件{id: 仿射 c}, 净增量{(i,j): 仿射})]。"""
    w, v, x, y = st
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = [aadd(ascale(v[j], G.lam[j]), *[w[(i, j)] for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]], A0(I[j])) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    gate_opts = [[]]
    regs = [R]
    combos = [([], R)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        nxt = []
        for gs, Rg in combos:
            d = aadd(vt[j], A0(-G.theta[j]))      # vt − θ
            for lab, R2 in split(Rg, [(1, [le0(ascale(d, -1))]), (0, [lt0(d)])]):
                nxt.append((gs + [lab], R2))
        combos = nxt
    out = []
    for g, Rg in combos:
        v2 = [A0(G.vreset[j]) if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        x2 = [G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)]
        y2 = [G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        ev0 = {}
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            ev0[('LEAK', (j,))] = ascale(v[j], G.lam[j] - 1)
            for i in range(G.n_in):
                if s[i]:
                    ev0[('SYN', (i, j))] = w[(i, j)]
            if I[j] != 0:
                ev0[('INJ', (j,))] = A0(I[j])
            if g[j]:
                ev0[('RESET', (j,))] = aadd(A0(G.vreset[j]), ascale(vt[j], -1))
                ev0[('YIN', (j,))] = A0(1)
            ev0[('YDEC', (j,))] = A0((G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j])
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            ev0[('XDEC', (i,))] = A0((G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i])
            if s[i]:
                ev0[('XIN', (i,))] = A0(1)
        Ssum = {}
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                parts = [w[(i, j)]]
                if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                    c = A0(G.Ap * x2[i]); ev0[('PLUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                    c = A0(-G.Am * y2[j]); ev0[('MINUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                    c = ascale(aadd(w[(i, j)], A0(-G.b[i][j])), -G.rho); ev0[('RELAX', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                Ssum[(i, j)] = aadd(*parts)
        pieces = [({}, Rg)]
        for k, S in Ssum.items():
            lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
            nxt = []
            for asg, Rp in pieces:
                opts = [('lo', [lt0(aadd(S, A0(-lo)))]),                                       # S < lo
                        ('mid', [le0(aadd(A0(lo), ascale(S, -1))), le0(aadd(S, A0(-hi)))]),     # lo ≤ S ≤ hi
                        ('hi', [lt0(aadd(A0(hi), ascale(S, -1)))])]                              # S > hi
                for lab, R2 in split(Rp, opts):
                    a2 = dict(asg); a2[k] = lab
                    nxt.append((a2, R2))
            pieces = nxt
        for asg, Rp in pieces:
            ev = dict(ev0)
            w2, net = {}, {}
            for k, S in Ssum.items():
                lab = asg[k]
                lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
                if lab == 'mid':
                    w2[k] = S
                else:
                    b = A0(lo if lab == 'lo' else hi)
                    w2[k] = b
                    ev[('CLIP', k)] = aadd(b, ascale(S, -1))
                net[k] = aadd(w2[k], ascale(w[k], -1))
            out.append((Rp, (w2, v2, x2, y2), ev, net))
    return out

def point_of(R, names):
    ok, x = feas(R)
    assert ok
    return {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}

def differs(R, a, c0):
    """R 上是否存在点使仿射 a ≠ c0；返回见证点或 None。"""
    if is_const(a):
        if a[0] != c0:
            ok, x = feas(R) if R else (True, {})
            return x
        return None
    for cons in (lt0(aadd(a, A0(-c0))), lt0(aadd(A0(c0), ascale(a, -1)))):
        ok, x = feas(simplify(R + [cons]))
        if ok:
            return x
    return None

# ---------- 实例与精确判定 ----------
class Decider:
    def __init__(self, I, budget=200000):
        self.I, self.G = I, I.G
        self.budget = budget
        self.sat = {}            # (k, t) 饱和的 T 位—拍 → 实际净增量
        for t in range(len(I.hist)):
            for e in I.evs[t].values():
                if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in I.T:
                    k = e['tgt']
                    self.sat[(k, t)] = I.pre[t + 1][0][k[1]][k[2]] - I.pre[t][0][k[1]][k[2]]
        self.fd1 = {}            # 事件 id → (u, 见证 dict) ；只记判 1 者
        self.fd1_net = {}
        self.complete_u = {}     # u → 是否在预算内穷尽
        self.verify_fail = 0
        self._run()
    def avail_T(self, u):
        return [k for k in self.I.T if self.I.is_avail(k, u)]
    def targets(self):
        I = self.I
        return {e['id'] for e in I.Wevents()}
    def _run(self):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        tgt = self.targets()
        us = [u for u in range(0, I.t1 + 1) if self.avail_T(u)]
        for u in us:
            S = self.avail_T(u)
            names = [('s',) + k for k in S]
            w0 = {(i, j): A0(I.pre[u][0][i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)}
            R0 = []
            for k, nm in zip(S, names):
                w0[(k[1], k[2])] = avar(nm)
                R0.append(le0(aadd(A0(G.b[k[1]][k[2]]), ascale(avar(nm), -1))))      # b − σ ≤ 0
                R0.append(le0(aadd(avar(nm), A0(-G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]))))               # σ − hi ≤ 0
            st0 = (w0, [A0(c) for c in I.pre[u][1]], list(I.pre[u][2]), list(I.pre[u][3]))
            nodes = 0
            stack = [(u, st0, R0)]
            complete = True
            while stack:
                t, st, R = stack.pop()
                nodes += 1
                if nodes > self.budget:
                    complete = False
                    break
                for R2, st2, ev, net in sym_step(G, st, R, I.hist[t], **I.kw):
                    for eid_s, e in I.evs[t].items():
                        if eid_s not in tgt or eid_s in self.fd1:
                            continue
                        key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
                        if key not in ev:
                            wit = point_of(R2, names)
                        else:
                            x = differs(R2, ev[key], e['c'])
                            wit = None if x is None else {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}
                        if wit is not None:
                            self._verify(u, S, names, wit, e)
                            self.fd1[eid_s] = (u, {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    for (k, tt), net0 in self.sat.items():
                        if tt == t and (k, t) not in self.fd1_net and t >= I.t0:
                            x = differs(R2, net[(k[1], k[2])], net0)
                            if x is not None:
                                self.fd1_net[(k, t)] = (u, {kk: x.get(nm, F(0)) for kk, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    if t + 1 <= I.t1:
                        stack.append((t + 1, st2, R2))
            ST.nodes += nodes
            self.complete_u[u] = complete
    def _verify(self, u, S, names, wit, e):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        assign = {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)}
        pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], assign), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
        if GK.same_event(evs, e):
            self.verify_fail += 1
    def fd1_value(self, e):
        if e['id'] in self.fd1:
            return 1
        us = [u for u in self.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
        return 0 if all(self.complete_u[u] for u in us) else GK.COV

def path_act(I, sources, e):
    """实际事件图（D13，只含实际发生的事件；读边、写边、结构位持存边）上自 sources = {(结点, 拍)} 是否可达 e。"""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, e['t'] + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == e['t']:
            return 1 if (e['reads'] & reach) else 0
        nxt = {k for k in reach if k[0] == 'w'}
        for ev in I.evs[t].values():
            if ev['reads'] & reach:
                nxt.add(ev['tgt'])
        reach = nxt
    return 0

def eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """返回 dict：值、档位、依据。domain='D1' 用 𝒟₁ 精确判定；'Dm' 另用引理 C、C′ 与钉位见证。"""
    if GK.path(I, e) == 0:
        return dict(v=0, tier='a', why='Path_slot = 0（R3）', fd=0)
    fd = D.fd1_value(e)
    wit = D.fd1.get(e['id'])
    if domain == 'Dm' and fd != 1:
        dv, why = GK.dep(I, e)
        if dv == 1:
            fd, wit = 1, None
        elif dv == 0 and fd == 0:
            fd = 0
        else:
            fd = GK.COV
    if fd == 1:
        if wit is not None:
            u, asg = wit
            src = {(k, u) for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
            why = f'𝒟₁ 见证 拍{u} ' + ','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}←{fr(val)}' for k, val in asg.items())
        else:
            src = {(k, u) for k in I.T for u in I.avail[k] if u <= e['t']}
            why = '钉位见证（𝒟_m）'
        if path_act(I, src, e):
            return dict(v=1, tier='+', why=why + '；实际图可达', fd=1)
        return dict(v='⊥_Open', tier='+?', why=why + '；实际图不可达（依赖只经未发生支路）', fd=1)
    if fd == 0:
        return dict(v=0 if bfd else '⊥_Over', tier='b', why=f'FD_{domain} = 0（全部分支不可行或常值相等）' + ('' if bfd else '；未申报 B_FD'), fd=0)
    return dict(v=GK.COV, tier='c', why='预算内未穷尽', fd=GK.COV)

def judge(I, D, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """Part、HDyn 判词（分列读法与联合读法；二者一致取之，否则 ⊥_Ind）。"""
    T = I.T
    rows = []
    hd_sep, hd_joint, pt_sep, pt_joint = [], [], [], []
    satk = {(k, t) for (k, t) in D.sat if t >= I.t0}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        ex = eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd, domain)
        a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
        ko = GK.ko_act(I.G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])[0]
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, ko=ko, **ex))
        if e['tgt'] in T:
            hd_sep.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
            if (e['tgt'], e['t']) not in satk:
                hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
        if e['car'] == RW.B:
            pt_sep.append(ex['v'])
            if not (e['tgt'] in T and (e['tgt'], e['t']) in satk):
                pt_joint.append(ex['v'])
    for (k, t) in satk:      # 联合事件：c = 净增量，读取 = 诸成员之并
        net0 = D.sat[(k, t)]
        mem = [e for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] == k]
        reads = frozenset().union(*[e['reads'] for e in mem])
        pseudo = dict(id=('NET', (k[1], k[2]), t), t=t, reads=reads, tgt=k, car=RW.B, c=net0)
        if net0 == 0:
            a = '⊥_Joint' if any(e['c'] != 0 for e in mem) else 0
        else:
            a = 1 if abs(net0) > EPS else 0
        if GK.path(I, pseudo) == 0:
            ev = 0
        elif (k, t) in D.fd1_net:
            u, asg = D.fd1_net[(k, t)]
            src = {(kk, u) for kk, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][kk[1]][kk[2]]}
            ev = 1 if path_act(I, src, pseudo) else '⊥_Open'
        else:
            ev = 0 if bfd else '⊥_Over'
        hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ev)); pt_joint.append(ev)
    hs, hj, ps, pj = GK.k_or(hd_sep), GK.k_or(hd_joint), GK.k_or(pt_sep), GK.k_or(pt_joint)
    H = hs if hs == hj else '⊥_Ind'
    P = ps if ps == pj else '⊥_Ind'
    return dict(rows=rows, HDyn=H, Part=P, HDyn_sep=hs, HDyn_joint=hj, nsat=len(satk))

def premises(I, D, res, bfd):
    """判 0 的前件清单（EF1—EF5 的对应物，正文 5.3）。"""
    base = ['MOD-R（R1：事件清单完备，模型结构）', 'R2/R3（读取集与槽位图闭合，模型结构）', 'REC', 'ENT', 'POS', 'BEAT-R（模型拍即 L 拍、ε 申报）']
    tiers = {r['tier'] for r in res['rows'] if r['v'] == 0}
    if 'b' in tiers:
        base += ['DOM[𝒟₁]（干预域事前申报）', 'B_FD[𝒟₁]（函数依赖读法的对象层对应）']
    if res['nsat']:
        base += ['IND_CLIP（夹限个别化，两读法一致才不需要）']
    return base

# ---------- 报告 ----------
def show(I, title, bfd=True, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.time()
    D = Decider(I)
    res = judge(I, D, bfd)
    resn = judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'【{title}】{I.name}')
    print(f'  W = 拍 {I.t0}—{I.t1}；T 首个可用拍 {min(min(I.avail[k]) for k in I.T if I.avail[k])}；'
          f'𝒟₁ 起点拍数 {len(D.complete_u)}，均在预算内穷尽：{all(D.complete_u.values())}；见证具体重跑核验失败 {D.verify_fail}；饱和位—拍 {len(D.sat)}')
    if verbose:
        print('  作用对象属 T 的 W 内事件：事件@拍 | c | a（读出/KO） | η | 档位 | 依据')
        for r in res['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            if e['tgt'] in I.T:
                print(f'    {e["id"][0]}{e["id"][1]}@{e["t"]} | {fr(e["c"])} | {r["a"]}/{r["ko"]} | {r["v"]} | {r["tier"]} | {r["why"]}')
    cnt = {}
    for r in res['rows']:
        if r['e']['car'] == RW.B:
            cnt[(r['tier'], str(r['v']))] = cnt.get((r['tier'], str(r['v'])), 0) + 1
    print('  B 内事件 κ 的档位分布：', '；'.join(f'{t}:{v}×{n}' for (t, v), n in sorted(cnt.items())))
    print(f'  判词（申报 B_FD[𝒟₁]）：Part = {res["Part"]}，HDyn = {res["HDyn"]}（分列 {res["HDyn_sep"]}／联合 {res["HDyn_joint"]}）')
    print(f'  判词（不申报 B_FD）：Part = {resn["Part"]}，HDyn = {resn["HDyn"]}')
    if res['HDyn'] == 0 or res['Part'] == 0:
        print('  判 0 的前件清单：', '；'.join(premises(I, D, res, bfd)))
    print(f'  （用时 {time.time() - t0:.1f} 秒）')
    return D, res, resn

def random_validation(n_inst=40, seed=7, samples=60):
    """定理 D1 的有限核验：随机 K_g 成员与史；对每一判 FD₁ = 0 的事件，在 𝒟₁ 内随机抽样设定，均不改变该事件；
    对每一判 1 者见证已具体重跑。另与 gate_criteria 的有限设定判据对照（后者判 1 ⇒ 此处判 1）。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(inst=0, events=0, fd0=0, fd1=0, samp=0, viol=0, vfail=0, gk1_not1=0)
    for _ in range(n_inst):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['hi'] = [[F(rng.choice([1, 2, 3]), 2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]
        form = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 3)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(3, 7))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
        st['inst'] += 1; st['vfail'] += D.verify_fail
        for e in I.Wevents():
            v = D.fd1_value(e)
            st['events'] += 1
            if v == 1:
                st['fd1'] += 1
            elif v == 0:
                st['fd0'] += 1
                us = [u for u in D.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
                for _s in range(samples // 10 if us else 0):
                    u = rng.choice(us)
                    asg = {k: G.b[k[1]][k[2]] + F(rng.randint(0, 48), 48) * (G.hi[k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]) for k in D.avail_T(u)}
                    _, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], asg), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
                    st['samp'] += 1
                    if not GK.same_event(evs, e):
                        st['viol'] += 1
            dv, _ = GK.dep(I, e)
            if dv == 1 and v != 1:
                # gate_criteria 的钉位见证属 𝒟_m，可在 𝒟₁ 之外；只统计其单拍见证不被 𝒟₁ 判 1 的情形
                runs, _, _ = I.cf()
                single = any((not r['lab'].startswith('钉位')) and r['u'] <= e['t'] and not GK.same_event(r['evs'], e) for r in runs)
                st['gk1_not1'] += single
    return st

def tmax_coverage():
    """T_max 归约的判词覆盖（正文定理 TM）：形成史使 4 个入口突触都成迹应，T_max = 4 位；
    对 T_max 的一切非空子集求 Part、HDyn，核单调性（T ⊆ T′ ⇒ 判词按真值序不减，且 1、0 不翻转）。"""
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    hist = [('AB', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0)), NONE, ('B', (F(1, 2), 0)), NONE, ('A', (F(1, 4), 0))]
    allT = [('w', i, 0) for i in range(4)]
    base = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=allT)
    end = base.pre[len(hist)]
    Tmax = [k for k in allT if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and base.form[k]]
    print(f'  史 (AB+教学 1, 静, 静, A, 静, B+教学 1/2, 静, A+教学 1/4)，W = 拍 3—7；T_max = {[f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Tmax]}')
    res = {}
    for r in range(1, len(Tmax) + 1):
        for Ts in itertools.combinations(Tmax, r):
            I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=list(Ts))
            D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
            j = judge(I, D, True)
            res[Ts] = (j['Part'], j['HDyn'], all(D.complete_u.values()), D.verify_fail)
    order = {0: 0, '⊥': 1, 1: 2}
    viol = 0
    for Ta in res:
        for Tb in res:
            if set(Ta) < set(Tb):
                for idx in (0, 1):
                    va, vb = res[Ta][idx], res[Tb][idx]
                    if va in (0, 1) and vb in (0, 1) and va > vb:
                        viol += 1
                    if va == 1 and vb != 1:
                        viol += 1
                    if vb == 0 and va != 0:
                        viol += 1
    for Ts, v in sorted(res.items(), key=lambda kv: len(kv[0])):
        print(f'    T = {{{",".join(f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Ts)}}}：Part = {v[0]}，HDyn = {v[1]}，穷尽 {v[2]}，见证核验失败 {v[3]}')
    print(f'  子集对 T ⊂ T′ 上单调性违例 {viol}（1 在扩大时保持为 1，0 在缩小时保持为 0）')
    return viol

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 申报史，W = (A+教学 1, 静, 静)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('形成 1 次；入口拍 A+教学；W = (B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4); S.W0 = F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    SAT = GK.Instance('W0 = 1/2、A₊ = 1/4；形成 (A+教学, 静, 静)×6，静置 8，W 为 (A,静,静,B,静,静)×3', Gs,
                      FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 8); S.W0 = F(1, 4)
    print('一、实例判词（𝒟₁ 精确判定；HistCond 三档负向；分列/联合两读法）')
    for I, t in ((P, '实例 P'), (N, '实例 N'), (O1, '实例 O1'), (O2, '实例 O2'), (SAT, '实例 SAT（夹限生效）')):
        show(I, t, verbose=(I is not SAT))
        print()
    print('二、定理 D1 的有限核验（随机 K_g 成员与史；FD₁ = 0 的事件在 𝒟₁ 内随机抽样均不变；判 1 的见证已具体重跑）')
    st = random_validation()
    print(f'  实例 {st["inst"]}，W 内事件 {st["events"]}：FD₁ = 1 者 {st["fd1"]}、= 0 者 {st["fd0"]}；抽样 {st["samp"]} 次，违例 {st["viol"]}；'
          f'见证重跑失败 {st["vfail"]}；有限设定判据的单拍见证未被 𝒟₁ 判 1 者 {st["gk1_not1"]}')
    print('三、T_max 归约的判词覆盖')
    tmax_coverage()
    print(f'四、线性可行性调用合计 {ST.lp} 次；符号分支结点合计 {ST.nodes}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.7　nonf\_encoding.py　命题 N1、N2 的前件读数与引理 Q 的核验（3.6）

~~~python
"""非 F 可编码性（正文 3.6 命题 N1、引理 Q、命题 N2）在冻结 SNN 上的数值前件与引理 Q 的有限核验。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 nonf_encoding.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
EPS = GK.EPS

def net_inc(G, o, sigma, k, wval, force_gate=None, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """位 k 在拍前值 w_k = wval 时的一拍净增量（夹后 − 夹前）；force_gate 给定时强制门控 j 取该值（用于求左极限）。"""
    i0, j0 = k[1], k[2]
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]; w[i0][j0] = wval
    v, x, y = o[1], o[2], o[3]
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = G.lam[j0] * v[j0] + sum(w[i][j0] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j0]
    g = (1 if vt >= G.theta[j0] else 0) if force_gate is None else force_gate
    x2 = G.dx[i0] * x[i0] + s[i0]
    y2 = G.dy[j0] * y[j0] + g
    pp = G.Ap * x2 if (plastic and bap and g) else F(0)
    pm = -G.Am * y2 if (plastic and bap and s[i0]) else F(0)
    pr = -G.rho * (wval - G.b[i0][j0]) if (plastic and G.rho != 0) else F(0)
    S = wval + pp + pm + pr
    return min(G.hi[i0][j0], max(G.lo[i0][j0], S)) - wval, pp

def crossings(I):
    """申报史中每一可用拍 t、每一可用且被门控读取的 T 位 k：越阈点 w*、跃变 J、增强分项 a。"""
    G = I.G
    out = []
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        sig = I.hist[t]; s = G.pat[sig[0]]; o = I.pre[t]
        for k in I.T:
            i0, j0 = k[1], k[2]
            if not I.is_avail(k, t) or not s[i0]:
                continue
            rest = G.lam[j0] * o[1][j0] + sum(o[0][i][j0] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in) if i != i0) + sig[1][j0]
            ws = G.theta[j0] - rest
            if not (G.b[i0][j0] < ws <= G.hi[i0][j0]):
                out.append(dict(t=t, k=k, ws=ws, inside=False))
                continue
            right, a = net_inc(G, o, sig, k, ws)
            left, _ = net_inc(G, o, sig, k, ws, force_gate=0)
            J = right - left
            out.append(dict(t=t, k=k, ws=ws, inside=True, J=J, a=a, Jint=(J / EPS).denominator == 1, inW=t >= I.t0))
    return out

def lemma_Q_check(n=3000, seed=20261102):
    """引理 Q 的有限核验：f(v) = Σ_m c_m(v)·[|c_m(v)| > ε]，c_m 为常数、线性或有理根二次式；在各 |c_m| = ε 的有理点处求左右极限之差。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    viol, pts = 0, 0
    for _ in range(n):
        mechs = []
        for _m in range(rng.randint(1, 6)):
            typ = rng.choice(['const', 'lin', 'quad'])
            if typ == 'const':
                mechs.append(('c', F(rng.randint(-20, 20), 100)))
            elif typ == 'lin':
                k = F(rng.choice([-1, 1]) * rng.randint(1, 30), 40)
                r = F(rng.randint(0, 175), 100)                    # 令 c(r) = ±ε
                sgn = rng.choice([-1, 1])
                mechs.append(('l', k, sgn * EPS - k * r))           # c(v) = b0 + k v
            else:
                q = F(rng.choice([-1, 1]) * rng.randint(1, 9), 10)
                r1, r2 = F(rng.randint(0, 175), 100), F(rng.randint(0, 175), 100)
                sgn = rng.choice([-1, 1])
                mechs.append(('q', q, r1, r2, sgn * EPS))           # c(v) = q (v − r1)(v − r2) + sgn·ε
        cands = set()
        for m in mechs:
            if m[0] == 'l':
                k, b0 = m[1], m[2]
                for tgt in (EPS, -EPS):
                    cands.add((tgt - b0) / k)
            elif m[0] == 'q':
                cands.add(m[2]); cands.add(m[3])
        def c_of(m, v):
            if m[0] == 'c': return m[1]
            if m[0] == 'l': return m[2] + m[1] * v
            return m[1] * (v - m[2]) * (v - m[3]) + m[4]
        def f_side(v, side):
            tot = F(0)
            for m in mechs:
                cv = c_of(m, v)
                if abs(cv) != EPS:
                    tot += cv if abs(cv) > EPS else 0
                else:   # 在 |c| = ε 处取单侧极限：看 v ± h（h 取足够小的有理数，单侧不越过其他根）
                    h = F(1, 10 ** 9)
                    cvh = c_of(m, v + side * h)
                    tot += cv if abs(cvh) > EPS else 0
            return tot
        for v in cands:
            pts += 1
            J = f_side(v, 1) - f_side(v, -1)
            if (J / EPS).denominator != 1:
                viol += 1
    return pts, viol

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    NONE = GK.NONE
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), len(FORM) + len(REST))
    print('一、实例 P 的首个窗内发放拍：N1、N2 的直接读数（LOC_w00，其余结点不变）')
    ts = [t for t in range(P.t0, P.t1 + 1) if P.gates[t][0]][0]
    o = P.pre[ts]; sig = P.hist[ts]; k = ('w', 0, 0)
    cr = [c for c in crossings(P) if c['t'] == ts and c['k'] == k][0]
    ws = cr['ws']
    print(f'  t* = {ts}；拍前 w00 = {GK.fr(o[0][0][0])}，w10 = {GK.fr(o[0][1][0])}，v0 = {o[1][0]}，x0 = {GK.fr(o[2][0])}，y0 = {GK.fr(o[3][0])}；字母 {sig[0]}')
    print(f'  越阈点 w* = θ − (λv0 + w10) = {ws} ≈ {float(ws):.6f}；LOC 合法域 [b, hi] = [{G.b[0][0]}, {G.hi[0][0]}]；w* 在内部：{G.b[0][0] < ws < G.hi[0][0]}')
    print('  w00 取值 | PLUS_00 | 净增量（夹后 − 拍前）')
    for wv in [G.b[0][0], (G.b[0][0] + ws) / 2, ws - F(1, 10 ** 6), ws, ws + F(1, 10 ** 6), o[0][0][0], F(1), G.hi[0][0]]:
        ev = RW.Ev(G, GK.setw(o, {k: wv}), sig, ts)
        pe = ev.get(('PLUS', (0, 0), ts))
        net, _ = net_inc(G, o, sig, k, wv)
        print(f'    {GK.fr(wv)} | {"发生 " + GK.fr(pe["c"]) if pe else "不发生"} | {GK.fr(net)}')
    print(f'  N1：增强分项 a = A₊x′₀ = {cr["a"]} ≈ {float(cr["a"]):.6f} > ε：{cr["a"] > EPS}。逐事件 F 机制的分项 c(v)·[|c(v)| > ε] 在 [w*, hi] 上恒等于 a，'
          '多项式 c 在无穷多点取 a，故 c ≡ a；在 v ∈ [b, w*) 处 |c| = a > ε 而入账 a，程序该处不发生——矛盾。')
    J = cr['J']
    print(f'  N2：净增量在 w* 处的跃变 J = {J} ≈ {float(J):.9f}；J/ε = {J / EPS} ≈ {float(J / EPS):.6f}，属 εℤ：{cr["Jint"]}')
    print(f'      解析：J = A₊x′₀ − A₋（发放使 y′ 加 1、抑制分项多 −A₋）= {G.Ap}·{GK.fr(G.dx[0] * o[2][0] + 1)} − {G.Am} = {G.Ap * (G.dx[0] * o[2][0] + 1) - G.Am}')
    print('      线性换标 s = c·z、ε′ = c·ε 不改变 J/ε；故结论对一切深度与迹应成正比的编码成立。')
    print('二、全部申报史上的越阈点与跃变（可用拍、门控读取该 T 位者）')
    hists = {
        'P（s1 冻结申报史）': P,
        'N（负判见证，见 po9_witness.py）': GK.Instance('N', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1),
    }
    for lab, I in hists.items():
        cs = crossings(I)
        ins = [c for c in cs if c['inside']]
        print(f'  {lab}：门控读取 T 的可用拍—位 {len(cs)} 处，其中越阈点在合法域内部 {len(ins)} 处；J ∈ εℤ 者 {sum(c["Jint"] for c in ins)} 处')
        for c in ins:
            print(f'    拍 {c["t"]}{"（W 内）" if c["inW"] else "（W 前）"} 位 w{c["k"][1]}{c["k"][2]}：w* = {GK.fr(c["ws"])}，a = {GK.fr(c["a"])}，J = {GK.fr(c["J"])}，J/ε = {float(c["J"] / EPS):.6f}')
    print('三、引理 Q 的有限核验（ε 截阈和的跃变属 εℤ）')
    pts, viol = lemma_Q_check()
    print(f'  随机机制组 3000 组、候选跃变点 {pts} 个，跃变不属 εℤ 者 {viol} 个')
    print('四、结论：在实例 P 的拍 26 上 N1、N2 的前件成立（a > ε；w* ∈ (b, hi)；J/ε ∉ ℤ）；N 的拍 5 同样成立。'
          '由正文 3.6 的证明，在 E1—E4（深度即迹应、BEAT、LOC 排他、精确）下，冻结 SNN 所在宿载不存在精确 F 编码（其余编码途径不在此结论内）；'
          '定理六、七不能经精确编码直接套用。')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.8　po9\_witness.py　实例 P、N、O1、O2 的对照，引理 C、C′ 的核验（5.3、7.5）

~~~python
"""实例 P、N、O1、O2 上第三篇判词路径与本篇判据的逐项对照（正文 7.5），引理 C、C′ 的有限核验（正文 5.3）。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 po9_witness.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import nonf_encoding as NF
EPS, NONE = GK.EPS, GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def wname(k):
    return f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}'

def report_instance(I, title, nonF=True):
    G = I.G
    print(f'【{title}】{I.name}')
    print(f'  史长 {len(I.hist)}；W = 拍 {I.t0}—{I.t1}；入口拍 {I.t0 - 1}；T = w00、w10；ε = {EPS}')
    print('  T 的形成拍（D16）：', {wname(k): I.form[k] for k in I.T}, '；首个可用拍（D17）：',
          {wname(k): (min(I.avail[k]) if I.avail[k] else None) for k in I.T})
    print('  W 内输出 0 发放拍：', [t for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 1) if I.gates[t][0]],
          '；W 内 z_T 范围：', {wname(k): (fr(min(GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2))),
                                       fr(max(GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)))) for k in I.T})
    ev = GK.evaluate(I, part_all=True)
    print(f'  合法设定运行 {ev["nruns"]} 条（含两条包络钉位）；钉位首次门控翻转拍：{ev["pins"]}')
    print('  作用对象属 T 的 W 内事件逐项求值：')
    print('    事件@拍 | 分项 c | KO 差 | a | Path | Dep | η（依据）')
    for r in ev['rows']:
        e = r['e']
        print(f'    {e["id"][0]}{e["id"][1]}@{e["t"]} | {fr(e["c"])} | {fr(r["d"]) if r["d"] is not None else "—"} | {r["a"]} | '
              f'{r["path"]} | {r["dep"]} | {r["eta"]}（{r["why"]}）')
    if not ev['rows']:
        print('    （无）')
    print(f'  本篇判据（主读法）：HDyn = {ev["HDyn"]}；Part = {ev["Part"]}（见证 {ev["Part_wit"]}）')
    print(f'  保守读法（超定处记 ⊥_Over）：HDyn = {ev["HDyn_cons"]}；Part = {ev["Part_cons"]}；作用有效而超定的 T 事件 {ev["n_over"]} 个')
    for k, (ret, form, zz) in ev['q'].items():
        print(f'  q({wname(k)})：窗末 z = {fr(zz)}，Ret = {ret}，Form = {form} ⇒ q = {ret and form}')
    print(f'  r：R 层 Mark 规则未立，判 {GK.RDEF}（本程序不求 r；r 见附录 A.14）；Ident = 1（POS，结构位即突触）；HistEv = 1（REC）；'
          f'Trace = {ev["Trace"]}；Q(T) = {ev["Q"]}')
    th = GK.third_path(I, nonF)
    print('  第三篇判词路径：')
    for s in th['steps']:
        print('    ·', s)
    print(f'  第三篇判词：q = {[th["q"][k] for k in I.T]}；r = {th["r"]}；Part = {th["Part"]}；HDyn = {th["HDyn"]}；Fld = {th["Fld"]}')
    for lab, ext in (('E1（入口为 B 内常数律）', False), ('E2（T 入口为界外脉冲）', True)):
        fl = GK.f_layer(I, I.T, ext)
        print(f'  488 型 F 编码 {lab}：Src = {fl["Src"]}，Ret = {fl["Ret"]}，Trace_F = {fl["Trace"]}，Part_F = {fl["Part"]}，'
              f'HDyn_F = {fl["HDyn"]}，Q_F = {fl["Q"]}；与程序 z_T 轨迹偏差 {len(fl["devs"])} 处'
              + (f'（首处拍 {fl["devs"][0][0]}，差 {fr(fl["devs"][0][2])}：阈下分项不入账，4.2）' if fl['devs'] else '')
              + (f'；{fl["dom_err"]}' if fl['dom_err'] else ''))
    return ev, th

def ef4_direct(I):
    """同拍合法局部设定改变 W 内门控的拍（EF4 所排除的「由迹应决定的发生」的直接读数）。"""
    runs, pins, _ = I.cf()
    found = []
    for r in runs:
        if r['lab'].startswith('钉位'):
            continue
        t = r['u']
        if t >= I.t0 and r['gates'][t] != I.gates[t]:
            found.append((t, r['ks'], r['lab'], I.gates[t][0], r['gates'][t][0]))
    return found

def pin_lemma_check(seed=20261102, n_inst=80):
    """引理 C 与 C′ 的有限核验：随机 K_g 成员（SNN 参数族变体）、随机史、随机 T、随机可设集合 A ⊆ T × 拍；
    两条包络钉位（A 上一律设 hi；A 上凡高于 b 即设 b）在 [u0, t] 上均无门控翻转时，
    任意合法多拍设定（A 内任意子集、值取 [b, hi] 内随机有理数）在 [u0, t] 上无门控翻转，且引理 C 覆盖种类的事件不变。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(windows=0, nopin=0, samples=0, viol=0, ev_checked=0)
    for _ in range(n_inst):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]
        L = 24
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(L)]
        o0 = ([[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)], [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        pre, evs, gates = GK.run_with(G, o0, hist)
        Tq = rng.sample([('w', i, j) for i in range(4) for j in range(2)], 2)
        Iq = type('Q', (), {})(); Iq.T = Tq; Iq.gates = gates; Iq.hist = hist; Iq.G = G
        for _w in range(4):
            u0 = rng.randint(0, 16); t = rng.randint(u0, L - 1)
            A = {(k, u) for k in Tq for u in range(u0, t + 1) if rng.random() < 0.7}
            if not A:
                continue
            st['windows'] += 1
            def pinrun(lab):
                def iv(tt, o):
                    a = {}
                    for k in Tq:
                        if (k, tt) in A:
                            if lab == 'hi':
                                a[k] = G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]
                            elif o[0][k[1]][k[2]] > G.b[k[1]][k[2]]:
                                a[k] = G.b[k[1]][k[2]]
                    return GK.setw(o, a) if a else o
                return GK.run_with(G, pre[u0], hist[u0:t + 1], t_start=u0, interv=iv)
            nop = all(all(gg[tt] == gates[tt] for tt in gg) for (_, _, gg) in (pinrun('hi'), pinrun('lo')))
            st['nopin'] += nop
            for _s in range(15):
                sel = [a for a in A if rng.random() < 0.4] or [next(iter(A))]
                vals = {a: G.b[a[0][1]][a[0][2]] + F(rng.randint(0, 64), 64) * (G.hi[a[0][1]][a[0][2]] - G.b[a[0][1]][a[0][2]]) for a in sel}
                def iv(tt, o):
                    a = {k: vals[(k, tt)] for k in Tq if (k, tt) in vals}
                    return GK.setw(o, a) if a else o
                _, ee, gg = GK.run_with(G, pre[u0], hist[u0:t + 1], t_start=u0, interv=iv)
                st['samples'] += 1
                if not nop:
                    continue
                flip = any(gg[tt] != gates[tt] for tt in gg)
                changed = False
                for tt in ee:
                    for eid in set(ee[tt]) | set(evs[tt]):
                        e = evs[tt].get(eid) or ee[tt].get(eid)
                        if GK.covered(Iq, e, u0):
                            st['ev_checked'] += 1
                            if (eid in ee[tt]) != (eid in evs[tt]) or (eid in ee[tt] and ee[tt][eid]['c'] != evs[tt][eid]['c']):
                                changed = True
                if flip or changed:
                    st['viol'] += 1
    return st

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史（FORM + REST + WIN）', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), len(FORM) + len(REST))
    N = GK.Instance('s1 参数；史 (A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                    [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 申报史，W = (A+教学 1, 静, 静)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], len(FORM) + len(REST))
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('s1 参数，形成 1 次；入口拍 A+教学；W = (B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], len(FORM1) + 8)
    print('〇、宿载的非 F 可编码性（nonf_encoding.py）：P 拍 26、N 拍 5 上 N1、N2 前件成立 ⇒ 第三篇 SEP 不能如实申报')
    for lab, I in (('P', P), ('N', N)):
        ins = [c for c in NF.crossings(I) if c['inside']]
        print(f'  {lab}：合法域内部越阈点 {len(ins)} 处，J ∉ εℤ 者 {sum(not c["Jint"] for c in ins)} 处')
    print()
    out = {}
    for I, lab, title in ((P, 'P', '实例 P：正判见证（门控越阈决定发生，强度不读迹应）'),
                          (N, 'N', '实例 N：负判见证（两读法一致）'),
                          (O1, 'O1', '实例 O1：同拍超定（读法分歧的展示）'),
                          (O2, 'O2', '实例 O2：经复位的跨拍超定（读法分歧的展示）')):
        out[lab] = report_instance(I, title)
        print()
    print('一、EF4 所排除情形的直接读数（同拍合法设定改变 W 内门控）')
    for I, lab in ((P, 'P'), (N, 'N'), (O1, 'O1'), (O2, 'O2')):
        f = ef4_direct(I)
        f0 = f[0] if f else None
        print(f'  {lab}：改变门控的拍 {sorted(set(x[0] for x in f))}；例：' +
              (f'拍 {f0[0]} 设 {",".join(wname(k) for k in f0[1])}←{f0[2]}，g0 {f0[3]}→{f0[4]}' if f0 else '无'))
    print('二、Dep = 1 ⇒ Path = 1（引理 R3 的推论）在全部已求值事件上的核对')
    bad = cnt = 0
    for I in (P, N, O1, O2):
        for e in I.Wevents():
            ev_, why, dv, pv, over = GK.eta(I, e)
            cnt += 1
            bad += (dv == 1 and pv == 0)
    print(f'  事件 {cnt} 个，违例 {bad}')
    print('三、对照总表（第三篇 / 本篇主读法 / 本篇保守读法 / 488 型 F 编码 E2）')
    for lab, I in (('P', P), ('N', N), ('O1', O1), ('O2', O2)):
        ev, th = out[lab]
        fl = GK.f_layer(I, I.T, True)
        print(f'  {lab}：Part {th["Part"]} / {ev["Part"]} / {ev["Part_cons"]} / {fl["Part"]}；'
              f'HDyn {th["HDyn"]} / {ev["HDyn"]} / {ev["HDyn_cons"]} / {fl["HDyn"]}；'
              f'q {[th["q"][k] for k in I.T]} / {ev["qv"]}；Fld {th["Fld"]} / Q(T) {ev["Q"]}')
    print("四、引理 C 与 C′（包络钉位引理及其复位扩展）的有限核验")
    st = pin_lemma_check()
    print(f'  随机实例 80、窗 {st["windows"]}，两钉位均无门控翻转者 {st["nopin"]}；随机合法多拍设定 {st["samples"]} 条；'
          f'覆盖种类事件比较 {st["ev_checked"]} 次；违例 {st["viol"]}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9　po4\_compat.py　交集相容对照的随机生成库（5.7）

~~~python
"""交集相容对照的随机生成与第三篇 4.6 判词在宿载状态上的独立实现（库，供附录 A.10 导入；其独立运行结果不作定理 C4′ 的核验）。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import r_layer_witness as RW
import decision as DC
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE

def in_omega(I, D):
    """（i）：每一可设拍起的符号运行是否单支且无夹限。"""
    G = I.G
    if any(e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' for e in I.Wevents()):
        return False
    for u in D.complete_u:
        S = D.avail_T(u)
        names = [('s',) + k for k in S]
        w0 = {(i, j): DC.A0(I.pre[u][0][i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)}
        R = []
        for k, nm in zip(S, names):
            w0[(k[1], k[2])] = DC.avar(nm)
            R.append(DC.le0(DC.aadd(DC.A0(G.b[k[1]][k[2]]), DC.ascale(DC.avar(nm), -1))))
            R.append(DC.le0(DC.aadd(DC.avar(nm), DC.A0(-G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]))))
        st = (w0, [DC.A0(c) for c in I.pre[u][1]], list(I.pre[u][2]), list(I.pre[u][3]))
        for t in range(u, I.t1 + 1):
            br = DC.sym_step(G, st, R, I.hist[t], **I.kw)
            if len(br) != 1 or any(k[0] == 'CLIP' for k in br[0][2]):
                return False
            R, st = br[0][0], br[0][1]
    return True

def ef4p(I, D):
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if D.fd1_value(e) == 1 and not any(k in I.T and I.is_avail(k, e['t']) for k in e['reads'] if k[0] == 'w'):
            return False
    return True

def third_46(I):
    """第三篇 4.6 的独立实现（Y₂，模型模式；INV-c、REC、ENT、POS 申报为真）。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    hd, pt, atoms = [], [], {}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        t = e['t']
        kind = e['id'][0]
        cand = [k for k in T if I.is_avail(k, t)]
        # 机制申报：作用于结构位者为可分机制；作用于辅助位者只有 SYN 是 OUT-f 读取过程，其余为 Rd = ∅ 的零常数律
        if e['tgt'][0] == 'w':
            a, d = GK.ko_act(G, I.pre[t], I.evs[t], e['id'])
            act = 1 if abs(d) > EPS else 0
            resp, tried = 0, 0
            for k in cand:
                for val in (G.b[k[1]][k[2]], G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]):
                    o2 = GK.setw(I.pre[t], {k: val})
                    if val == I.pre[t][0][k[1]][k[2]]:
                        continue
                    ev2 = RW.Ev(G, o2, I.hist[t], t, **I.kw)
                    # 截阈入账项 ĉ = c·[|c| > ε]（〔三〕133）
                    c0 = e['c'] if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
                    c1 = ev2[e['id']]['c'] if e['id'] in ev2 else 0
                    c1 = c1 if abs(c1) > EPS else 0
                    tried += 1
                    resp = resp or (c1 != c0)
                    break
            if resp:
                hc = 1
            elif not cand:
                hc = 0
            elif act == 1:
                hc = 0 if tried == len(cand) else '⊥_Obs'
            else:
                hc = '⊥_Sub'
            atoms[e['id']] = (act, hc)
            if e['tgt'] in T:
                hd.append(GK.k_and(act, hc))
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(hc if act == 1 or hc == 1 else (0 if not cand else hc))
        else:
            rdT = [k for k in e['reads'] if k in T and I.is_avail(k, t)]
            if kind in ('SYN', 'RESET'):
                # OUT-f：SYN 读出 = w；RESET 读出 = v_reset − (λv + Σ w s + I)，对每一所读 w 逐位单射；Rd = 所读结构位（定理六（三））
                cond = 1 if rdT else 0
            elif kind == 'YIN':
                cond = '⊥_Sub' if rdT else 0              # 读出恒为 1，不满足 OUT-f；门控不变时无响应（4.6（3））
            else:
                cond = 0                                  # LEAK、YDEC、XDEC、XIN、INJ 不读结构位：Rd = ∅ 的零常数律
            atoms[e['id']] = (0, cond)
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(cond)
    return GK.k_or(pt), GK.k_or(hd), atoms

def main(n_try=400, seed=20261103):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(tried=0, omega=0, agree_part=0, agree_hd=0, atom_cmp=0, atom_dis=0, tierb=0, nontriv_hd1=0, nontriv_part1=0)
    dis = []
    for _ in range(n_try):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['theta'] = [F(rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]))] * 2
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 4)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3)]
        form = [('A', (F(3), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 2)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        st['tried'] += 1
        D = DC.Decider(I, budget=20000)
        if not in_omega(I, D) or not ef4p(I, D):
            continue
        st['omega'] += 1
        ours = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        P3, H3, atoms = third_46(I)
        st['agree_part'] += (ours['Part'] == P3)
        st['agree_hd'] += (ours['HDyn'] == H3)
        st['conf'] = st.get('conf', 0) + ((ours['Part'], P3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0))) + ((ours['HDyn'], H3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0)))
        st['refine'] = st.get('refine', 0) + (isinstance(P3, str) and ours['Part'] in (0, 1)) + (isinstance(H3, str) and ours['HDyn'] in (0, 1))
        st['nontriv_hd1'] += (H3 == 1)
        st['nontriv_part1'] += (P3 == 1)
        for r in ours['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            a3, h3 = atoms[e['id']]
            if h3 in (0, 1):
                st['atom_cmp'] += 1
                st['tierb'] += (r['tier'] == 'b')
                if r['v'] != h3:
                    st['atom_dis'] += 1
                    dis.append((e['id'], r['v'], h3, r['tier']))
        if ours['Part'] != P3 or ours['HDyn'] != H3:
            dis.append(('判词', ours['Part'], P3, ours['HDyn'], H3))
    print('交集相容的对照（随机 K_g 成员与史；只取逐项满足 Ω_F 与 EF4′ 者）')
    print(f'  有可用位的随机实例 {st["tried"]}，其中属 Ω_F ∩ EF4′ 者 {st["omega"]}')
    print(f'  Part 相同 {st["agree_part"]}/{st["omega"]}（第三篇判 1 者 {st["nontriv_part1"]}）；HDyn 相同 {st["agree_hd"]}/{st["omega"]}（第三篇判 1 者 {st["nontriv_hd1"]}）')
    print(f'  判词 1 与 0 相冲突 {st.get("conf", 0)} 处；第三篇 ⊥（⊥_Sub 等）而本篇已判者 {st.get("refine", 0)} 处（信息序细化，非冲突）')
    print(f'  逐原子比较（第三篇取 0、1 者）{st["atom_cmp"]} 个，不同 {st["atom_dis"]}；其中本篇以第 (b) 档（FD = 0，B_FD）签 0 者 {st["tierb"]}')
    for d in dis[:10]:
        print('   不一致：', d)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.10　po4\_ns.py　截取反例、引理 NS、定理 C4′ 子域的重核（5.7）

~~~python
"""交集相容（正文 5.7）：分辨率截取反例的逐值复现与回归、引理 NS（轨迹相同 ⇔ 每拍每位被截取分项之和为 0）的核验、定理 C4′ 子域 Ω_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS_act ∩ NN_act 上的重核。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 po4_ns.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import r_layer_witness as RW
import decision as DC
import po4_compat as PC
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def f_run(I):
    """E_F 的实际 F 运行（只取 T 位；数理式 4.2：只入账 |c| > ε 的分项）。返回 (sF[t][k], 每拍每位截取和, F 形成记录, 非负违例)。"""
    G = I.G
    sF = {0: {k: GK.zk(G, I.pre[0], k) for k in I.T}}
    drop, formF, neg = {}, {k: list(x for x in I.form[k] if x < 0) for k in I.T}, []
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        nxt = dict(sF[t])
        for k in I.T:
            dsum = F(0)
            for e in I.evs[t].values():
                if e['tgt'] != k:
                    continue
                kind = e['id'][0]
                if kind == 'RELAX':
                    c = -I.G.rho * sF[t][k]
                elif kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS'):
                    c = e['c']                       # 申报发生的常数律（取实际史的值）
                elif kind == 'CLIP':
                    c = e['c']                       # Ω_F 内不出现
                else:
                    continue
                hc = e['c']
                if 0 < abs(hc) <= EPS:
                    dsum += hc
                if abs(c) > EPS:
                    if sF[t][k] <= EPS < sF[t][k] + c:
                        formF[k].append(t)
                    nxt[k] = nxt[k] + c
            drop[(t, k)] = dsum
            if nxt[k] < 0:
                neg.append((t + 1, k))
        sF[t + 1] = nxt
    return sF, drop, formF, neg

def ns_act(I, drop):
    return all(v == 0 for v in drop.values())

def same_traj(I, sF):
    return all(sF[t][k] == GK.zk(I.G, I.pre[t], k) for t in sF for k in I.T)

def third_46_F(I, sF, formF):
    """第三篇 4.6 在 E_F 自身 F 运行状态上的求值（可用位、局部响应、敲除差都取 F 状态；E_F 的律：PLUS/MINUS 常数，RELAX = −ρ·s，
    SYN、RESET 为 OUT-f 读取（Rd = 所读结构位），YIN 读出恒常，其余辅助位过程为 Rd = ∅ 的零常数律）。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    av = lambda k, t: sF[t][k] > EPS and any(u < t for u in formF[k])
    hd, pt, atoms = [], [], {}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        t, kind = e['t'], e['id'][0]
        cand = [k for k in T if av(k, t)]
        if e['tgt'][0] == 'w':
            k0 = e['tgt']
            if kind == 'RELAX' and k0 in T:
                c0 = -G.rho * sF[t][k0]
                cut = lambda c: c if abs(c) > EPS else 0
                act = 1 if abs(c0) > EPS else 0
                resp = False
                if k0 in cand:                       # 每一候选位施一次合法扰动（先设深度 0，与原值同则设 hi）
                    s_new = F(0) if sF[t][k0] != 0 else G.hi[k0[1]][k0[2]] - G.b[k0[1]][k0[2]]
                    resp = cut(-G.rho * s_new) != cut(c0)
            else:                                    # PLUS、MINUS（常数律）、T 外位上的过程：不读 T 中候选位，无响应
                act = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
                resp = False
            hc = 1 if resp else (0 if not cand else (0 if act == 1 else '⊥_Sub'))
            atoms[e['id']] = (act, hc)
            if e['tgt'] in T:
                hd.append(GK.k_and(act, hc))
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(hc if act == 1 or hc == 1 else (0 if not cand else hc))
        else:
            rdT = [k for k in e['reads'] if k in T and av(k, t)]
            if kind in ('SYN', 'RESET'):
                cond = 1 if rdT else 0
            elif kind == 'YIN':
                cond = '⊥_Sub' if rdT else 0
            else:
                cond = 0
            atoms[e['id']] = (0, cond)
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(cond)
    return GK.k_or(pt), GK.k_or(hd), atoms

def ent_instance(name, G, hist, t0, T, o0):
    I = GK.Instance(name, G, hist, t0, T=T, o0=o0)
    for k in T:
        if GK.zk(G, o0, k) > EPS:
            I.form[k] = [-1] + I.form[k]
    I.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(hist) + 1) if GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in I.form[k])] for k in T}
    return I

def section_counter(S):
    print('一、截取反例的逐值复现（ε = 1/100，z = 3/200，ρ = 1/2，静置；T = {w00}，形成史按 ENT 登记于入口拍 −1）')
    G = GC.Gate(rho=F(1, 2), **GC.snn_params(S))
    o0 = G.init()
    o0[0][0][0] = G.b[0][0] + F(3, 200)
    hist = [NONE] * 4
    I = ent_instance('反例', G, hist, 0, [('w', 0, 0)], o0)
    sF, drop, formF, neg = f_run(I)
    k = ('w', 0, 0)
    e0 = I.evs[0][('RELAX', (0, 0), 0)]
    print(f'  拍 0：宿载 RELAX00 分项 c = {fr(e0["c"])}，|c| = {fr(abs(e0["c"]))} < ε；无发放、无其他写入、无夹限（夹限事件 {sum(1 for e in I.evs[0].values() if e["id"][0] == "CLIP")} 个）')
    print(f'  拍 1：宿载 z = {fr(GK.zk(G, I.pre[1], k))}；E_F 的 F 运行 s = {fr(sF[1][k])}；差 {fr(sF[1][k] - GK.zk(G, I.pre[1], k))}')
    for t in range(len(hist) + 1):
        zh = GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k)
        avh = I.is_avail(k, t)
        avf = sF[t][k] > EPS and any(u < t for u in formF[k])
        print(f'    拍 {t}：宿载 z = {fr(zh)}（可用 {int(avh)}）｜F s = {fr(sF[t][k])}（可用 {int(avf)}）｜本拍截取和 {fr(drop.get((t, k), F(0)))}')
    zend, send = GK.zk(G, I.pre[len(hist)], k), sF[len(hist)][k]
    print(f'  窗末 Ret：宿载 {int(zend > EPS)}（z = {fr(zend)}），E_F {int(send > EPS)}（s = {fr(send)}）——可用位与 Ret 自拍 1 起分歧，F 一侧此后恒为 3/200（每拍 −3/400 都被截取）')
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    print(f'  该实例的子域判定：Ω_F（单支、无夹限）{PC.in_omega(I, D)}；EF4′ {PC.ef4p(I, D)}；NS_act {ns_act(I, drop)}；轨迹相同 {same_traj(I, sF)}')
    print('  结论：反例满足 Ω_F——等式「E_F 与宿载在 𝒟₁ 内逐拍一致」所依的唯一条件——而轨迹不一致，故该等式不成立。'
          '（反例本身不在 EF4′ 内：拍 1 的 RELAX00 依拍 0 的设定而不读拍 1 的可用位；该逐拍一致等式并未以 EF4′ 为前提。）')
    # 回归二：可用位分歧导致第三篇（在 F 状态上求值）与本篇（宿载）判词不同的实例
    G3 = GC.Gate(rho=F(1, 2), **dict(GC.snn_params(S), theta=[F(3)] * 2))
    o3 = G3.init(); o3[0][0][0] = G3.b[0][0] + F(3, 200)
    I3 = ent_instance('回归二', G3, [NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1, [('w', 0, 0)], o3)
    sF3, drop3, formF3, _ = f_run(I3)
    D3 = DC.Decider(I3)
    j3 = DC.judge(I3, D3, True)
    P3, H3, _ = third_46_F(I3, sF3, formF3)
    print(f'  回归二（θ = 3 使门控恒不发放；史 (静, A)，W = 拍 1）：宿载拍 1 z = {fr(GK.zk(G3, I3.pre[1], ("w", 0, 0)))}（不可用），F s = {fr(sF3[1][("w", 0, 0)])}（可用）；'
          f'第三篇在 F 运行上 Part = {P3}；本篇（宿载）Part = {j3["Part"]}；子域判定：Ω_F {PC.in_omega(I3, D3)}、EF4′ {PC.ef4p(I3, D3)}、NS_act {ns_act(I3, drop3)}')
    print('  读法：回归二不在 EF4′ 内（拍 1 的 SYN00 依拍 0 的设定而不读拍 1 的可用位），子域 Ω_F ∩ EF4′ 已排除它；它说明一旦轨迹分歧，两侧的「可用位」指不同的东西，判词比较失去对象。')
    print()

def section_recheck(S, n_try=400, seed=20261103):
    print('二、交集相容重核（po4_compat.py 的同一随机生成与种子；第三篇 4.6 改在 E_F 自身的 F 运行状态上求值）')
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    st = dict(tried=0, omega=0, ns=0, nn=0, good=0, eq_traj_ns=0, conf_good=0, agreeP=0, agreeH=0, atom_cmp=0, atom_dis=0,
              ns_fail_conf=0, ns_fail=0, retdiv=0, avdiv=0, rc=0)
    ex = []
    for _ in range(n_try):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['theta'] = [F(rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]))] * 2
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 4)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3)]
        form = [('A', (F(3), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 2)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        st['tried'] += 1
        D = DC.Decider(I, budget=20000)
        if not PC.in_omega(I, D) or not PC.ef4p(I, D):
            continue
        st['omega'] += 1
        sF, drop, formF, neg = f_run(I)
        ns, nn = ns_act(I, drop), not neg
        rc = all(e['c'] == 0 or abs(e['c']) > EPS for t in range(len(I.hist)) for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] in I.T)
        st['ns'] += ns; st['nn'] += nn; st['rc'] += rc
        tr = same_traj(I, sF)
        assert tr == ns, '引理 NS 的等价不成立'
        ours = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        P3, H3, atoms = third_46_F(I, sF, formF)
        conf = ((ours['Part'], P3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0))) + ((ours['HDyn'], H3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0)))
        if not ns:
            st['ns_fail'] += 1
            st['ns_fail_conf'] += (conf > 0)
            end = len(I.hist)
            st['retdiv'] += any((GK.zk(G, I.pre[end], k) > EPS) != (sF[end][k] > EPS) for k in I.T)
            st['avdiv'] += any(I.is_avail(k, t) != (sF[t][k] > EPS and any(u < t for u in formF[k])) for k in I.T for t in range(end + 1))
            if conf:
                ex.insert(0, ('冲突', [str(G.lam[0]), str(G.Ap), str(G.Am), str(G.rho), str(G.theta[0]), str(G.b[0][0])],
                              [(a, str(I_[0])) for a, I_ in I.hist], ours['Part'], P3, ours['HDyn'], H3))
            if len(ex) < 4:
                t1 = min(t for (t, k), v in drop.items() if v != 0)
                ex.append((t1, [(fr(v)) for (t, k), v in sorted(drop.items()) if v != 0][:2], ours['Part'], P3, ours['HDyn'], H3))
        if ns and nn:
            st['good'] += 1
            # 在 NS ∧ NN 上 F 状态与宿载状态相同，F 状态求值应与 po4_compat 的宿载状态求值逐原子相同
            P0, H0, atoms0 = PC.third_46(I)
            st['eq_traj_ns'] += (P0 == P3 and H0 == H3)
            st['conf_good'] += conf
            st['agreeP'] += (ours['Part'] == P3); st['agreeH'] += (ours['HDyn'] == H3)
            for r in ours['rows']:
                a3, h3 = atoms[r['e']['id']]
                if h3 in (0, 1):
                    st['atom_cmp'] += 1
                    st['atom_dis'] += (r['v'] != h3)
    print(f'  有可用位的随机实例 {st["tried"]}；子域 Ω_F ∩ EF4′ {st["omega"]}')
    print(f'  其中 NS_act 成立 {st["ns"]}、NN_act（E_F 的 F 运行非负，D20）成立 {st["nn"]}、两者都成立 {st["good"]}；逐分项分辨率相容 RC（T 上每一分项为 0 或 |c| > ε）成立 {st["rc"]}')
    print(f'  引理 NS（轨迹相同 ⇔ NS_act）在全部 {st["omega"]} 例上成立（程序断言）')
    print(f'  NS_act 不成立 {st["ns_fail"]} 例：窗末 Ret 分歧 {st["retdiv"]}、可用位分歧 {st["avdiv"]}、判词 1—0 冲突（本篇宿载 vs 第三篇在 F 运行上）{st["ns_fail_conf"]}')
    for x in ex[:4]:
        if x[0] == '冲突':
            print(f'    冲突例：λ、A₊、A₋、ρ、θ、b = {x[1]}；史（入口, 教学0）{x[2]}；本篇 Part {x[3]}／第三篇(F) {x[4]}；本篇 HDyn {x[5]}／第三篇(F) {x[6]}')
        else:
            print(f'    例：首个非零截取和在拍 {x[0]}（{x[1]}）；本篇 Part {x[2]}／第三篇(F) {x[3]}；本篇 HDyn {x[4]}／第三篇(F) {x[5]}')
    print(f'  交集相容子域 Ω_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS_act ∩ NN_act：{st["good"]} 例；F 状态求值与宿载状态求值相同 {st["eq_traj_ns"]}/{st["good"]}；'
          f'Part 相同 {st["agreeP"]}、HDyn 相同 {st["agreeH"]}；1—0 冲突 {st["conf_good"]}；逐原子（第三篇取 0、1）{st["atom_cmp"]} 个，不同 {st["atom_dis"]}')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h}（与冻结值相符）')
    section_counter(S)
    section_recheck(S)
    print('结束。以上为有限核验与精确读数；引理 NS 与定理 C4′ 的证明见正文 5.7。')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.11　boundary\_po8.py　引理 IMP-g 的逐见证核对、定理 B2 的判词、闭环对照（5.5、5.6）

~~~python
"""回读与界内自反馈型（正文 5.5、5.6）：程序宿载上 IMP 由模型结构成立的逐见证核对；开环 𝒦_g 中界内自反馈型判词（定理 B2）；SNN 途径与切断；闭环对照（定理 B3）。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 boundary_po8.py
"""
import sys, random, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import decision as DC
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def wname(k):
    return f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}'

def ename(e):
    return f"{e['id'][0]}{''.join(str(c) for c in e['id'][1])}@{e['t']}"

def nname(n):
    return (n[0] + ''.join(str(c) for c in n[1:]))

# ---------------- 实际事件图上的路径重建 ----------------
def act_path(I, sources, e):
    """Γ_act（D13：读边、写边、结构位持存边；只含实际发生的事件）上自 sources = {(结点, 拍)} 到 e 的一条路径（结点序列），无则 None。"""
    if not sources:
        return None
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    parent = {}
    reach = {}
    sources = sorted(sources)
    for (k, u) in sources:
        parent[('S', k, u)] = None
    cur = set()
    for t in range(t0, e['t'] + 1):
        for (k, u) in sources:
            if u == t and k not in cur:
                cur.add(k); parent.setdefault(('S', k, t), None)
        if t == e['t']:
            hit = sorted(k for k in e['reads'] if k in cur)
            if not hit:
                return None
            node = ('S', hit[0], t)
            path = [('E', e['id'], e['car'])]
            while node is not None:
                path.append(node)
                node = parent[node]
            return list(reversed(path))
        nxt = set()
        for k in sorted(cur):
            if k[0] == 'w':
                nxt.add(k)
                parent.setdefault(('S', k, t + 1), ('S', k, t))
        for ev in I.evs[t].values():
            hit = sorted(k for k in ev['reads'] if k in cur)
            if hit:
                pe = ('E', ev['id'], ev['car'])
                parent.setdefault(pe, ('S', hit[0], t))
                nxt.add(ev['tgt'])
                parent.setdefault(('S', ev['tgt'], t + 1), pe)
        cur = nxt
    return None

def path_str(p):
    out = []
    for n in p:
        if n[0] == 'S':
            out.append(f'({nname(n[1])},{n[2]})')
        else:
            out.append(f"{n[1][0]}{''.join(map(str, n[1][1]))}@{n[1][2]}[{n[2]}]")
    return ' → '.join(out)

def path_in_B(p):
    """路径全在 B 内：状态结点皆属 B（定义 4：w、v、x、y 都是 B 的结点），事件结点载体为 B。"""
    return all(n[0] == 'S' or n[2] == RW.B for n in p)

# ---------------- 一、回读 ----------------
def imp_check(I, D):
    rows = []
    for eid, (u, asg) in D.fd1.items():
        t = eid[2]
        e = I.evs[t][eid]
        Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
        set_state = GK.setw(I.pre[u], asg)
        # IMP-2 排他
        diff = [(k) for k in RW.nodes(I.G) if RW.node_val(set_state, k) != RW.node_val(I.pre[u], k)]
        imp2 = set(diff) <= Q and all(k[0] == 'w' for k in diff)
        # IMP-1 实际读取（实际事件图首跳为读边）
        p = act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
        imp1 = p is not None
        # IMP-3 同一转移：重跑用同一 G.step 与同一申报字母序列
        pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(I.G, set_state, I.hist[u:], t_start=u, **I.kw)
        imp3 = True
        # IMP-4 无旁路：改变的事件都在槽位超图前向可达集内（R3）
        R = RW.reach_forward(I.G, I.hist, {(k, u) for k in Q}, **I.kw)
        bad = 0
        for tt in range(u, len(I.hist)):
            for e0 in I.evs[tt].values():
                if not GK.same_event(evs2, e0) and not (e0['reads'] & R[tt]):
                    bad += 1
        changed = not GK.same_event(evs2, e)
        rows.append(dict(e=e, u=u, Q=Q, imp1=imp1, imp2=imp2, imp3=imp3, imp4=(bad == 0), changed=changed, path=p))
    return rows

def section_po7(insts):
    print('一、回读：程序宿载上 IMP 由模型结构成立（逐见证核对）')
    tot = dict(n=0, i1=0, i2=0, i3=0, i4=0, ch=0)
    for nm, I in insts:
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        rows = imp_check(I, D)
        for r in rows:
            tot['n'] += 1
            tot['i1'] += r['imp1']; tot['i2'] += r['imp2']; tot['i3'] += r['imp3']; tot['i4'] += r['imp4']; tot['ch'] += r['changed']
        ex = [r for r in rows if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T][:1]
        print(f'  {nm}：𝒟₁ 正判见证 {len(rows)} 个；IMP-1 {sum(r["imp1"] for r in rows)}、IMP-2 {sum(r["imp2"] for r in rows)}、'
              f'IMP-3 {sum(r["imp3"] for r in rows)}、IMP-4 {sum(r["imp4"] for r in rows)}；见证重跑使事件改变 {sum(r["changed"] for r in rows)}')
        for r in ex:
            print(f'    例：{ename(r["e"])}，拍 {r["u"]} 设 {",".join(wname(k) for k in sorted(r["Q"]))}；实际路径 {path_str(r["path"])[:260]}')
~~~
