~~~python
    print(f'  合计 {tot["n"]} 个见证：四项全成立 {min(tot["i1"], tot["i2"], tot["i3"], tot["i4"])}；见证有效 {tot["ch"]}')
    print('  读法：四项都由程序结构给出（设定直接改写过程所读的状态变量；K 不变；字母不变；差异只沿槽位超图传递）；'
          '这是〔三〕200 行「模型模式中 IMP 由模型结构成立」在门控类上的逐见证核对，不推及任何物质实现（Dom4）。')
    print()

# ---------------- 二、界内自反馈型 ----------------
def inb_verdict(I, D, bfd=True):
    """定理 B2：开环 K_g 中，界内自反馈型判词 = HDyn 判词；正判另给出实际路径并核其全在 B 内。"""
    res = DC.judge(I, D, bfd)
    wit = None
    for r in res['rows']:
        e = r['e']
        if e['tgt'] in I.T and r['a'] == 1 and r['v'] == 1 and e['car'] == RW.B:
            u, asg = D.fd1[e['id']]
            Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
            p = act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
            wit = (e, u, Q, p)
            break
    return res, wit

def section_po8(G, insts):
    print('二、界内自反馈型（开环 K_g，定理 B2）')
    for nm, I in insts:
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        res, wit = inb_verdict(I, D, True)
        resn, _ = inb_verdict(I, D, False)
        extT = [e for e in I.Wevents() if e['tgt'] in I.T and e['car'] != RW.B]
        line = f'  {nm}：HDyn = {res["HDyn"]}（不申报 B_FD：{resn["HDyn"]}）；作用于 T 的界外载体事件 {len(extT)} 个；界内自反馈型 = {res["HDyn"]}（不申报 B_FD：{resn["HDyn"]}）'
        print(line)
        if wit:
            e, u, Q, p = wit
            print(f'    见证 {ename(e)}（拍 {u} 设 {",".join(wname(k) for k in sorted(Q))}）；实际路径全在 B 内：{path_in_B(p)}；路径长 {len(p)}')
        if res['HDyn'] == 0:
            print('    判 0 的前件：' + '；'.join(DC.premises(I, D, res, True)) + '；开环申报（字母为申报的外生序列，无界外通路）')
    # SNN 途径「突触 → 输出神经元 → 反传至同一突触」：实例 P 拍 26 的同拍见证
    P = insts[0][1]
    t = 26
    e = P.evs[t][('PLUS', (0, 0), t)]
    asg = {('w', 0, 0): G.b[0][0]}
    pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(P.pre[t], asg), P.hist[t:t + 1], t_start=t)
    p = act_path(P, {(('w', 0, 0), t)}, e)
    rec = G.step(P.pre[t], P.hist[t])[1]
    rec2 = G.step(GK.setw(P.pre[t], asg), P.hist[t])[1]
    print(f'  SNN 途径（实例 P 拍 26，同拍合法设定 w00 ← b）：实际 vt0 = {fr(rec["vt"][0])}、g0 = {rec["g"][0]}；设定后 vt0 = {fr(rec2["vt"][0])}、g0 = {rec2["g"][0]}；'
          f'PLUS00@26 设定后{"不发生" if ("PLUS", (0, 0), t) not in evs2.get(t, {}) else "仍发生"}')
    print(f'    实际路径：{path_str(p)}；全在 B 内：{path_in_B(p)}')
    print('    细化读法：(w00,26) 经 SYN00@26（突触传递，读 w00）汇入输出 0 的积分 vt0 → 门控 g0（输出神经元发放）→ 反传到达突触（bap）→ PLUS00@26 写回同一突触 w00；'
          '各环节的载体都是 B 内单元（突触、输出神经元），申报中无任何界外结点在此路径上。')
    # 途径切断：按构造
    o_r = P.pre[P.t0]                                   # 形成（可塑性、反传都开）与静置之后的状态
    for flag, kw in (('bap = 0', dict(bap=False)), ('plastic = 0', dict(plastic=False))):
        # 只在 W 内切断（同 SNN-v2 的对照）：W 自拍 0 记录，形成史按 ENT 登记于入口拍 −1
        I2 = GK.Instance(P.name + f'（W 内 {flag}）', G, P.hist[P.t0:], 0, o0=o_r, **kw)
        for k in I2.T:
            I2.form[k] = [-1] + I2.form[k]
        I2.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(I2.hist) + 1)
                        if GK.zk(G, I2.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in I2.form[k])] for k in I2.T}
        nT = sum(1 for e2 in I2.Wevents() if e2['tgt'] in I2.T)
        D2 = DC.Decider(I2)
        j2 = DC.judge(I2, D2, True)
        print(f'  W 内切断 {flag}（形成史按 ENT 登记）：W 内作用于 T 的事件 {nT} 个；HDyn = {j2["HDyn"]}，界内自反馈型 = {j2["HDyn"]}，Part = {j2["Part"]}（按定义 2（6）的构造：增强与抑制只在 plastic ∧ bap 下发生，ρ = 0；不作独立发现）')
    print()

# ---------------- 三、定理 B2 的有限核验 ----------------
def random_gate(rng):
    n_in, n_out = 3, 2
    pat = {'none': (0, 0, 0), 'a': (1, 0, 0), 'b': (0, 1, 1), 'ab': (1, 1, 1)}
    lam = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for _ in range(n_out)]
    b = [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
    return GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, lam, [F(1)] * n_out, [F(0)] * n_out, [F(1, 2)] * n_in, [F(1, 2)] * n_out,
                   F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8), F(rng.randint(0, 2), 16), F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1]), 10), b,
                   [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)], [[F(rng.choice([1, 2])) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)])

def section_b2():
    print('三、定理 B2 的有限核验（随机开环 K_g 成员）')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_inst = n_wit = n_inB = n_inj_mid = n_extT = 0
    n_eq = 0
    while n_inst < 40:
        G = random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(5, 8)
        form = [(rng.choice(['a', 'b', 'ab']), (F(1), F(rng.choice([0, 1])))) for _ in range(2)]
        win = [(rng.choice(list(G.pat)), tuple(F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2]), 4) for _ in range(2))) for _ in range(L - 2)]
        h = form + win
        T = rng.sample([('w', i, j) for i in range(3) for j in range(2)], 2)
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 2, T=T)
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in T):
            continue
        D = DC.Decider(I, budget=30000)
        res = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        n_inst += 1
        n_extT += sum(1 for e in I.Wevents() if e['tgt'] in T and e['car'] != RW.B)
        for r in res['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            if e['tgt'] in T and r['v'] == 1 and e['id'] in D.fd1:
                u, asg = D.fd1[e['id']]
                Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
                p = act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
                n_wit += 1
                if p is not None and path_in_B(p):
                    n_inB += 1
                if p is not None and any(n[0] == 'E' and n[1][0] == 'INJ' for n in p[:-1]):
                    n_inj_mid += 1
        n_eq += 1
    print(f'  实例 {n_inst}：作用于 T 的界外载体事件 {n_extT} 个；正判见证 {n_wit} 个，其实际路径全在 B 内 {n_inB} 个，INJ 作中间结点 {n_inj_mid} 次')
    print('  读法：INJ 的读取集为空，不可能有入边，故不能作路径的中间结点；作用于 T 的事件只有 PLUS、MINUS、RELAX、CLIP，载体都在 B（定义 5）。')
    print()

# ---------------- 四、闭环对照 ----------------
def run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0, interv=None, clamp=None, I_first=(0, 0)):
    """闭环：环境（界外载体 E）在拍 t 读输出 0 的发放 g0(t)，拍 t+1 向输出 0 注入 I0 = 1（g0 = 1）或 0。
    clamp 给定时字母钳为 clamp 序列（外钳：界外分量取实际值，与设定无关）。返回 (逐拍拍前态, 逐拍事件, 实现的字母序列)。"""
    pre, evs, letters = {}, {}, []
    Inext = I_first
    for n, a in enumerate(inputs):
        t = t0 + n
        if interv is not None:
            o = interv(t, o)
        sig = clamp[n] if clamp is not None else (a, Inext)
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t)
        o2, rec = G.step(o, sig)
        pre[t], evs[t] = o, e
        letters.append(sig)
        Inext = (F(1) if rec['g'][0] else F(0), F(0))
        o = o2
    return pre, evs, letters

def section_closed(G):
    print('四、闭环对照（定理 B3 的范围）')
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    o = G.init()
    for sig in FORM + REST:
        o, _ = G.step(o, sig)
    t0 = 26
    inputs = ['A', 'none']
    pre, evs, letters = run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0)
    e = evs[27].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 27))
    print(f'  申报：形成与静置同 s1（拍 0—25）；拍 26 字母 A，拍 27 静置；环境闭环注入：拍 27 的 I0 = {fr(letters[1][1][0])}（因拍 26 输出 0 发放）；W = 拍 27；T = {{w00, w10}}')
    print(f'  实际：PLUS00@27 {"发生，c = " + fr(e["c"]) if e else "不发生"}')
    asg = {('w', 0, 0): G.b[0][0], ('w', 1, 0): G.b[1][0]}
    iv = lambda t, oo: GK.setw(oo, asg) if t == 26 else oo
    preL, evsL, lettersL = run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0, interv=iv)
    preC, evsC, _ = run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0, interv=iv, clamp=letters)
    eL = evsL[27].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 27)); eC = evsC[27].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 27))
    print(f'  拍 26 设 w00、w10 ← b：环境活动时 拍 26 g0 = {int(("RESET", (0,), 26) in evsL[26])}，拍 27 I0 = {fr(lettersL[1][1][0])}，PLUS00@27 {"发生" if eL else "不发生"}（响应：有）')
    print(f'                         外钳（字母钳为实际值）时 PLUS00@27 {"发生，c = " + fr(eC["c"]) if eC else "不发生"}（响应：{"无" if (eC and eC["c"] == e["c"]) else "有"}）')
    print('  实际路径：(w00,26) → 门控 g0(26) → 环境 E 读发放并于拍 27 注入（界外载体）→ INJ0@27 → 门控 g0(27) → PLUS00@27：经界外，χ_e 型；HDyn（闭环实例）= 1。')
    # 钳定实例（字母取实际实现序列、开环申报）上的 HDyn 档位 = 闭环实例的界内自反馈型（定理 B3）
    Icl = GK.Instance('钳定实例', G, FORM + REST + letters, 27)
    D = DC.Decider(Icl)
    j = DC.judge(Icl, D, True); jn = DC.judge(Icl, D, False)
    rows = [r for r in j['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in Icl.T]
    print('  钳定实例（开环申报，字母 = 实际实现序列）上作用于 T 的 W 内事件：' + '；'.join(f'{ename(r["e"])} a={r["a"]} η={r["v"]}（档 {r["tier"]}）' for r in rows))
    print(f'  ⇒ 闭环实例的界内自反馈型：申报 B_FD^钳 时 {j["HDyn"]}，不申报时 {jn["HDyn"]}；闭环实例的 HDyn = 1（经界外途径）。')
    print('  读法：外钳下无响应只说明钳定实例的函数依赖为 0（第（b）档）；按第三篇引理十三与负向三档，不申报对应前件时保持 ⊥，不以「外钳无响应」判 0。'
          '把环境划入 B 或改为开环外生申报，都是另一实例（〔三〕68）。')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h}（与冻结值相符）')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 申报史，W = (A+教学 1, 静, 静)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('形成 1 次；W = (B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4); S.W0 = F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    SAT = GK.Instance('W0 = 1/2、A₊ = 1/4（夹限生效）', Gs, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 8); S.W0 = F(1, 4)
    insts = [('实例 P', P), ('实例 N', N), ('实例 O1', O1), ('实例 O2', O2), ('实例 SAT', SAT)]
    t0 = time.time()
    section_po7(insts[:2])
    section_po8(G, insts)
    section_b2()
    section_closed(G)
    print(f'结束。以上为有限核验与精确读数；一般命题的证明见正文 5.5、5.6。')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.12　b2\_codes.py　定理 B2 的逐事件同值核验（5.6）

~~~python
"""定理 B2 的逐事件同值核验：开环 𝒦_g 中界内自反馈型与 HDyn 的候选域、外钳重跑与原因码逐项相同（正文 5.6）。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 b2_codes.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import r_layer_witness as RW
import decision as DC
import boundary_po8 as BP
NONE = GK.NONE

def compare(I, bfd):
    D = DC.Decider(I, budget=30000)
    res = DC.judge(I, D, bfd)
    hd = {r['e']['id']: (r['a'], r['v']) for r in res['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T}
    dom_h = set(hd)
    inb, dom_b = {}, set()
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['tgt'] in I.T and e['car'] == RW.B:
            dom_b.add(e['id'])
            ex = DC.eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd)
            v = ex['v']
            if v == 1:
                u, asg = D.fd1[e['id']]
                Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
                p = BP.act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
                v = 1 if (p is not None and BP.path_in_B(p)) else '⊥_ext'
            inb[e['id']] = (1 if abs(e['c']) > GK.EPS else 0, v)
    same = sum(1 for k in dom_h if inb.get(k) == hd[k])
    codes = sorted({str(v[1]) for v in hd.values()})
    J = lambda d: GK.k_or(GK.k_and(a, v) for a, v in d.values())
    return dom_h == dom_b, same, len(dom_h), J(hd), J(inb), codes, res['nsat']

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h}（与冻结值相符）')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    insts = [('实例 P', GK.Instance('', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)),
             ('实例 N', GK.Instance('', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)),
             ('实例 O1', GK.Instance('', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)),
             ('实例 O2', GK.Instance('', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] +
                                    [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11))]
    print('一、四个 SNN 实例（分列读法；申报与不申报 B_FD 两列）')
    for nm, I in insts:
        for bfd in (True, False):
            d, same, n, H, B, codes, nsat = compare(I, bfd)
            print(f'  {nm}（{"申报" if bfd else "不申报"} B_FD）：候选域相同 {d}；逐事件 (a, η) 同值（η 含原因码）{same}/{n}；HDyn = {H}，界内自反馈型 = {B}；出现的 η 值 {codes}')
    print('二、随机开环 K_g 成员（同 boundary_po8 第三节的生成，种子 20261102）')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_inst = tot = same_t = dom_ok = cod = 0
    allcodes = set()
    while n_inst < 40:
        G2 = BP.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(5, 8)
        form = [(rng.choice(['a', 'b', 'ab']), (F(1), F(rng.choice([0, 1])))) for _ in range(2)]
        win = [(rng.choice(list(G2.pat)), tuple(F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2]), 4) for _ in range(2))) for _ in range(L - 2)]
        T = rng.sample([('w', i, j) for i in range(3) for j in range(2)], 2)
        I = GK.Instance('', G2, form + win, 2, T=T)
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in T):
            continue
        n_inst += 1
        for bfd in (True, False):
            d, same, n, H, B, codes, nsat = compare(I, bfd)
            dom_ok += d; tot += n; same_t += same; cod += (H == B)
            allcodes |= set(codes)
    print(f'  实例 40 × 两列：候选域相同 {dom_ok}/80；逐事件 (a, η) 同值 {same_t}/{tot}；判词同值 {cod}/80；出现的 η 值 {sorted(allcodes)}')
    print('  说明：夹限饱和位—拍的联合读法（D9）两套判词取同一组联合事件，本程序只比较分列读法原子。')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.13　source\_r3.py　来源的严格子域分类、撤除域、静息条件与 F 模型对照库（5.4）

~~~python
"""来源的严格子域分类库（正文 5.4 定理 SUB 所比较的分类：S*₀、S*₁、⊥_aux、S 等）、撤除域、静息条件 P4R 与 P4Z、F 模型对照器（供附录 A.14、A.15 导入）。
「⊥_aux」指 P4R 成立而 P4Z 不成立的写入（严格子域外）；正文按判据 PS 对这类写入逐例判定（正文 7.5（五））。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def wname(k):
    return f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}'

def ename(e):
    return f"{e['id'][0]}{''.join(str(c) for c in e['id'][1])}@{e['t']}"

# ---------------- 定义 S-1：入场分量（申报的界外字母 σ = (a, I) 中非零的分量） ----------------
def entry_comps(G, sigma):
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    return [('in', i) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]] + [('I', j) for j in range(G.n_out) if I[j] != 0]

def removals(G, sigma):
    """撤除域 𝒟_σ(t)：把实际字母的若干非零分量撤为零（入口取申报字母表中模式逐位不大于实际模式者；教学电流逐分量取原值或 0）。
    撤除只作用于申报的界外信号，不改状态、不改过程律（Dom3：检验，不属侵入）。"""
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    subs = [a for a, p in G.pat.items() if all(p[i] <= s[i] for i in range(G.n_in))]
    Is = list(itertools.product(*[sorted({I[j], 0}, key=str) for j in range(G.n_out)]))
    out = []
    for a in subs:
        for Iv in Is:
            sig = (a, tuple(Iv))
            if sig != (name, tuple(I)):
                out.append(sig)
    return out

# ---------------- 定义 S-2：拍内实际来源链（入场分量 → 实际事件 → 发放结点 → 写入），不经结构位结点 ----------------
def tick_chain(G, o, sigma, evs, carried, t):
    """返回 (R, gate, carried_next, why, gwhy)。
    R：本拍由入场分量经实际边可达的事件 (kind, idx) → 其链起点集（入场分量 (种类, 下标, 拍)）；每条边都携带非零贡献：
      字母边：('in', i) → XIN_i、SYN_ij（发生由 s_i 定）、MINUS_ij（发生由 s_i 定）、PLUS_ij（分项经 x′_i = d·x_i + s_i）；('I', j) → INJ_j；
      汇入发放结点 γ_j（只在 g_j = 1 时存在）：自 SYN_ij（c ≠ 0）、INJ_j、LEAK_j（v_j 携入场影响且 c ≠ 0）；
      γ_j → RESET_j、YIN_j、PLUS_ij（发生）、MINUS_ij（分项经 y′_j = d·y_j + g_j）；
      辅助位跨拍：carried（结点 → 起点集）中的 v、x、y 经 LEAK、XDEC、YDEC 与本拍新入（XIN、YIN、RESET）传到下一拍。
    链不经结构位 w 的结点（SYN 读 w 是汇入边，不是链的传递边；RELAX 只读 w，永不在链上）。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    ev = {(e['id'][0], e['id'][1]): e for e in evs.values()}
    R, why = {}, {}
    def add(key, reason, orig):
        if key in ev and orig:
            if key not in R:
                why[key] = reason
            R.setdefault(key, set()).update(orig)
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        if s[i]:
            add(('XIN', (i,)), f'入口{i}', {('in', i, t)})
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                add(('SYN', (i, j)), f'入口{i}经突触 w{i}{j} 传递', {('in', i, t)})
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if I[j] != 0:
            add(('INJ', (j,)), f'教学电流 I{j}', {('I', j, t)})
        if ('v', j) in carried and ('LEAK', (j,)) in ev and ev[('LEAK', (j,))]['c'] != 0:
            add(('LEAK', (j,)), f'v{j} 携先前入场影响', carried[('v', j)])
        if ('y', j) in carried and ('YDEC', (j,)) in ev and ev[('YDEC', (j,))]['c'] != 0:
            add(('YDEC', (j,)), f'y{j} 携先前入场影响', carried[('y', j)])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        if ('x', i) in carried and ev[('XDEC', (i,))]['c'] != 0:
            add(('XDEC', (i,)), f'x{i} 携先前入场影响', carried[('x', i)])
    gate, gwhy = {}, {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:                                  # g_j = 1
            src = [k for k in R if ev[k]['c'] != 0 and
                   ((k[0] == 'SYN' and k[1][1] == j) or (k[0] in ('INJ', 'LEAK') and k[1][0] == j))]
            if src:
                gate[j] = set().union(*[R[k] for k in src]); gwhy[j] = src
    for j in gate:
        add(('RESET', (j,)), f'发放 γ{j}', gate[j]); add(('YIN', (j,)), f'发放 γ{j}', gate[j])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] * x[i] != 0 else set())
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            key = ('PLUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                orig = (gate.get(j, set())) | xo
                rs = []
                if j in gate: rs.append(f'发放 γ{j} 经反传到达突触（发生）')
                if xo: rs.append(f'x′{i} 携入场（分项）')
                add(key, '；'.join(rs), orig)
            key = ('MINUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                yo = gate.get(j, set()) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] * y[j] != 0 else set())
                add(key, f'入口{i}（发生）' + (f'；y′{j} 携入场（分项）' if yo else ''), {('in', i, t)} | yo)
            key = ('CLIP', (i, j))
            mem = [m for m in (('PLUS', (i, j)), ('MINUS', (i, j))) if m in R]
            if key in ev and mem:
                add(key, '夹限成员在链上', set().union(*[R[m] for m in mem]))
    C2 = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:
            if j in gate:
                C2[('v', j)] = set(gate[j])
        else:
            cs = [k for k in [('LEAK', (j,)), ('INJ', (j,))] + [('SYN', (i, j)) for i in range(G.n_in)] if k in R and ev[k]['c'] != 0]
            if cs:
                C2[('v', j)] = set().union(*[R[k] for k in cs])
        yo = set(gate.get(j, set())) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] * y[j] != 0 else set())
        if yo:
            C2[('y', j)] = yo
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] * x[i] != 0 else set())
        if xo:
            C2[('x', i)] = xo
    return R, gate, C2, why, gwhy

def chains(I):
    """对实例 I 的实际史逐拍求 Ch_0（只用本拍入场）与 Ch_aux（经辅助位跨拍），各为 事件键 → 链起点集。"""
    ch0, chaux, info = {}, {}, {}
    carried = {}
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        R0, g0, _, why0, gw0 = tick_chain(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], {}, t)
        Ra, ga, carried, whya, gwa = tick_chain(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], carried, t)
        ch0[t], chaux[t], info[t] = R0, Ra, (why0, gw0, whya, gwa)
    return ch0, chaux, info

def changed_comps(G, sig, sig2, t):
    """撤除 sig → sig2 在拍 t 改了值的入场分量。"""
    return {('in', i, t) for i in range(G.n_in) if G.pat[sig[0]][i] != G.pat[sig2[0]][i]} | \
           {('I', j, t) for j in range(G.n_out) if sig[1][j] != sig2[1][j]}

# ---------------- 定义 S-3：入场依赖 SigDep（撤除申报信号的分量，事件的发生或分项改变） ----------------
def sigdep0(I, e, orig=None):
    """同拍撤除见证；orig 给定时只取「所撤分量与 e 的链起点相交」者（依赖与链出自同一组入场分量）。"""
    t = e['t']
    wits = []
    for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[t]):
        if orig is not None and not (changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[t], sig, t) & orig):
            continue
        evs2 = RW.Ev(I.G, I.pre[t], sig, t, **I.kw)
        if e['id'] not in evs2:
            wits.append((sig, '不发生'))
        elif evs2[e['id']]['c'] != e['c']:
            wits.append((sig, f"分项 {fr(e['c'])}→{fr(evs2[e['id']]['c'])}"))
    return wits

def sigdep_m(I, e, lookback=6, orig=None):
    """撤除较早拍 u（t − lookback ≤ u < t）的信号分量，自 u 重跑到 t(e)，事件改变（入场经辅助位的跨拍依赖）。"""
    t = e['t']
    for u in range(max(0, t - lookback), t):
        for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[u]):
            if orig is not None and not (changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[u], sig, u) & orig):
                continue
            pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(I.G, I.pre[u], [sig] + I.hist[u + 1:t + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
            if not GK.same_event(evs, e):
                return (u, sig)
    return None

def null_letter(G):
    return ('none', tuple(0 for _ in range(G.n_out)))

def p5_null(I, t, k):
    """前件 P5：本拍字母换为静置（撤除一切入场）时，结构位 k 上无任何有效事件——申报初始组织与宿载规则单独不在 k 上留下有效改变。
    （只涉 e 的作用位 k，不涉 T 的其余位，故 Mark_R(k) 与 T 无关，r_R 对 T 为 ∃ 型单调。）"""
    evs2 = RW.Ev(I.G, I.pre[t], null_letter(I.G), t, **I.kw)
    return not any(e2['tgt'] == k and abs(e2['c']) > EPS for e2 in evs2.values())

def trace_free(I, t):
    """S*₁ 的前件 P4′：拍 0—t 的拍前状态中 X 的一切结构位都无已留迹应（z ≤ ε）——此前的一切活动只出自入场与申报初始组织。"""
    G = I.G
    X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    return all(GK.zk(G, I.pre[u], k) <= EPS for u in range(t + 1) for k in X)

def p5_all(I, t, k, o_init):
    """S*₁ 的前件 P5′：自申报初态起、拍 0—t 一律换为静置字母时，拍 t 上 k 无有效事件。"""
    pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(I.G, o_init, [null_letter(I.G)] * (t + 1), **I.kw)
    return not any(e2['tgt'] == k and abs(e2['c']) > EPS for e2 in evs[t].values())

def rest_state(I, o):
    """P4R：o 是静置字母下的不动点（K(o, 静置) = o，且静置下无任何非零分项），且 X 中无已留迹应。
    本函数只检查一步不动与静置分项为 0；辅助状态是否取无记忆基准另由 zero_aux（P4Z）逐项核验。"""
    G = I.G
    o2, rec = G.step(o, null_letter(G), **I.kw)
    evs = RW.Ev(G, o, null_letter(G), 0, **I.kw)
    X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    return same_state(o2, o) and all(e['c'] == 0 for e in evs.values()) and all(GK.zk(G, o, k) <= EPS for k in X)

def zero_aux(I, o):
    """P4Z：承重辅助状态逐项取无记忆基准 0（v_j = 0、x_i = 0、y_j = 0），即定义 1 的初态值（附录 A.1 snn_core.py 的初态）。
    x、y 是资格迹（x = Σ d^k s_{t−k}，y 同），v 是积分的入场；取 0 即「无任何先前入场或发放在辅助位上留下值」。逐项核验，不由静置不动性推出。"""
    return all(c == 0 for c in o[1]) and all(c == 0 for c in o[2]) and all(c == 0 for c in o[3])

def same_state(o1, o2):
    return o1[0] == o2[0] and list(o1[1]) == list(o2[1]) and list(o1[2]) == list(o2[2]) and list(o1[3]) == list(o2[3])

def classify(I, ch0, chaux, o_init, lookback=6, tmax=None):
    """对 Hist(T) 中（t ≤ t₁）作用于 T 且有效的每一事件求来源分类。
    S*₀：Ch_0 ∧ 同拍撤除见证（所撤分量属链起点）∧ P4R ∧ P4Z（拍前为静息态且辅助态逐项为 0）∧ P5（静置下 k 上无有效事件）；
    S：Ch_aux ∧ 撤除见证（同拍或较早拍，所撤分量属链起点）；⊥_σOpen：有撤除依赖而无出自所撤分量的实际链；
    ⊥_σOver：有实际链而声明的撤除域内无依赖；无链：Ch_aux 不可达。"""
    rows = []
    tmax = I.t1 if tmax is None else tmax
    for t in range(0, tmax + 1):
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] not in I.T or abs(e['c']) <= EPS:
                continue
            key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
            o0 = ch0[t].get(key, set()); oa = chaux[t].get(key, set())
            c0, ca = bool(o0), bool(oa)
            sd0 = sigdep0(I, e, o0) if c0 else []
            sdA = sigdep0(I, e, oa) if ca else []
            sdm = None if (sd0 or sdA or not ca) else sigdep_m(I, e, lookback, oa)
            p4r = rest_state(I, I.pre[t])
            p4 = p4r and zero_aux(I, I.pre[t])
            p5 = p5_null(I, t, e['tgt'])
            if c0 and sd0 and p4 and p5:
                cls = 'S*₀'
            elif ca and (sdA or sdm) and rest_state(I, I.pre[0]) and zero_aux(I, I.pre[0]) and trace_free(I, t) and p5_all(I, t, e['tgt'], I.pre[0]):
                cls = 'S*₁'
            elif c0 and sd0 and p4r and p5 and not zero_aux(I, I.pre[t]):
                cls = '⊥_aux'                    # 静置不动而辅助态非零：来源子域外（严格子域外），不判 0
            elif ca and (sdA or sdm):
                cls = 'S'
            elif (w0 := sigdep0(I, e)) or (wm := sigdep_m(I, e, lookback)):
                wit = (t, w0[0][0]) if w0 else wm
                cls = '⊥_σOpen' if wfree_reach(I, e, wit) else '续行'
            elif ca:
                cls = '⊥_σOver'
            else:
                cls = '无链'
            rows.append(dict(e=e, c0=c0, ca=ca, sd0=sd0 or sdA, sdm=sdm, p4=p4, p5=p5, cls=cls, orig=oa))
    return rows

def wfree_reach(I, e, wit):
    """撤除依赖是否可能不经结构位传递：在去掉结构位结点的槽位超图上，自所撤分量能否到达 e。
    不可达时，由 R3σ 的同一论证，依赖只能经结构位（已留迹应）传递，e 属场内续行（如弛豫读本位迹应），不是入场依赖。"""
    u, sig = wit
    comps = {(k, i) for (k, i, _u) in changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[u], sig, u)}
    aff, R = entry_slot_reach(I.G, I.hist, u, comps, noW=True, **I.kw)
    return aff(e) if e['t'] == u else bool(e['reads'] & R.get(e['t'], frozenset()))

def r_verdict(rows):
    """r_R(T)：有 S* 判 1（定理，引理 S*）；无 S* 而有 S 记「RS 读法下 1」；一切有效 T 事件都「无链」判 0（引理 S⁻，前件 REC、ENT、R1、EF1′ 与读法 N）；
    其余 ⊥。"""
    if any(r['cls'] == 'S*₀' for r in rows):
        return '1[严格]', 'S*₀'
    if any(r['cls'] == 'S*₁' for r in rows):
        return '1[严格, EF1′]', 'S*₁'
    if any(r['cls'] == '⊥_aux' for r in rows):
        return '⊥_aux', '来源子域外（静止辅助态非零）'
    if any(r['cls'] == 'S' for r in rows):
        return '1（RS 读法）/ ⊥（不申报 RS）', 'S'
    if all(r['cls'] in ('无链', '续行') for r in rows):
        return 0, 'S⁻'
    return '⊥', '⊥'

def show_chain(I, info, e, which='0'):
    t = e['t']
    why0, gw0, whya, gwa = info[t]
    why, gw = (why0, gw0) if which == '0' else (whya, gwa)
    key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
    parts = [why.get(key, '—')]
    j = e['id'][1][1] if e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') else None
    if j is not None and j in gw:
        parts.append('γ%d 的入场汇入：' % j + '、'.join(f"{k[0]}{''.join(map(str, k[1]))}（{fr(I.evs[t][(k[0], k[1], t)]['c'])}）" for k in sorted(gw[j])))
    return '；'.join(parts)

# ---------------- 一、二：SNN 实例 ----------------
def section_snn(G):
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                    [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    o_init = G.init()
    out = {}
    print('一、SNN 入场信号—承受介质—形成的发放—结构改变—传递路径（实例 P 拍 0，逐项精确读数）')
    t = 0
    sig = P.hist[t]
    o = P.pre[t]
    rec = G.step(o, sig)[1]
    print(f'  申报：B = 入口单元 4、输出单元 2、突触 8；界外信号 = 入口刺激序列与教学电流（〔core〕3—4 行）；T = {{w00, w10}}；ε = {EPS}')
    print(f'  拍 0 拍前状态 = 申报初态（w = b = {G.b[0][0]}，v = x = y = 0）：{same_state(o, o_init)}；是静息态（P4R）：{rest_state(P, o)}；辅助态逐项为无记忆基准 0（P4Z）：{zero_aux(P, o)}')
    print(f'  （1）入场信号：字母 {sig[0]}（入口模式 {G.pat[sig[0]]}）＋教学电流 I = ({fr(F(sig[1][0]))}, {fr(F(sig[1][1]))})；入场分量 {entry_comps(G, sig)}')
    ev = P.evs[t]
    def c(kind, idx):
        k = (kind, idx, t)
        return fr(ev[k]['c']) if k in ev else '无'
    print(f'  （2）承受介质：入口单元 0、1 接受刺激（XIN0 = {c("XIN", (0,))}、XIN1 = {c("XIN", (1,))} 写入资格迹 x0、x1）；'
          f'突触 w00、w10 传递（SYN00 = {c("SYN", (0, 0))}、SYN10 = {c("SYN", (1, 0))}，传递量即基线 b，非迹应）；'
          f'输出单元 0 的膜承受教学电流（INJ0 = {c("INJ", (0,))}，载体界外）')
    print(f'  （3）形成的发放：输出 0 积分 vt0 = λ·0 + {fr(G.b[0][0])} + {fr(G.b[1][0])} + 1 = {fr(rec["vt"][0])} ≥ θ = {G.theta[0]}，g0 = {rec["g"][0]}；'
          f'输出 1 积分 vt1 = {fr(rec["vt"][1])} < θ，g1 = {rec["g"][1]}')
    print(f'  （4）结构改变：PLUS00 = A₊·x′0 = {c("PLUS", (0, 0))}，MINUS00 = −A₋·y′0 = {c("MINUS", (0, 0))}；PLUS10 = {c("PLUS", (1, 0))}，MINUS10 = {c("MINUS", (1, 0))}；'
          f'w00：{fr(P.pre[0][0][0][0])} → {fr(P.pre[1][0][0][0])}，迹应 z = {fr(P.pre[1][0][0][0] - G.b[0][0])} > ε，形成记录拍 {P.form[("w", 0, 0)][:1]}')
    ch0, chaux, info = chains(P)
    for key in [('PLUS', (0, 0)), ('MINUS', (0, 0))]:
        e = ev[(key[0], key[1], t)]
        print(f'  （5）传递路径 {ename(e)}：{show_chain(P, info, e)}；链起点 {sorted(ch0[t][key])}')
    print('      即：刺激 → 入口单元（XIN）→ 经突触 w00、w10 传递（SYN）与教学电流（INJ）汇入输出 0 膜电位 → 越阈发放 γ0 → 反传到达突触 → '
          '与前突触资格迹 x′0 相乘写入 w00；整条链在拍 0 内完成，不经任何已留迹应。')
    for r in sigdep0(P, ev[('PLUS', (0, 0), t)], ch0[t][('PLUS', (0, 0))]):
        print(f'      撤除 {r[0][0]}＋I=({fr(F(r[0][1][0]))},{fr(F(r[0][1][1]))})：PLUS00@0 {r[1]}')
    print(f'      静置字母（撤除一切入场）下 w00、w10 上有效事件：{"无" if (p5_null(P, 0, ("w", 0, 0)) and p5_null(P, 0, ("w", 1, 0))) else "有"}（P5）')
    print()
    print('二、来源认证（引理 S*、S、S⁻）')
    for I, nm in ((P, '实例 P'), (N, '实例 N')):
        ch0, chaux, info = chains(I)
        rows = classify(I, ch0, chaux, o_init)
        rv = r_verdict(rows)
        cnt = {}
        for r in rows:
            cnt[r['cls']] = cnt.get(r['cls'], 0) + 1
        print(f'  【{nm}】{I.name}；Hist(T) = 拍 0—{I.t1}，作用于 T 的有效事件 {len(rows)} 个，分类：' +
              '；'.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(cnt.items())))
        for r in rows[:6]:
            e = r['e']
            print(f"    {ename(e)} | c = {fr(e['c'])} | Ch_0 {int(r['c0'])} Ch_aux {int(r['ca'])} | SigDep_0 {len(r['sd0'])} 个撤除见证 | "
                  f"P4 {int(r['p4'])} P5 {int(r['p5'])} | {r['cls']}")
        sform = sorted({r['e']['t'] for r in rows if r['cls'] == 'S'})
        if sform:
            print(f'    S 类（弱读法 RS：入场与已留迹应共同决定）所在拍：{sform[:12]}{" …" if len(sform) > 12 else ""}')
        print(f'    r_R(T) = {rv[0]}（依据 {rv[1]}）')
        out[nm] = (I, rows, rv)
    # 对照 C1：迹应只由申报初始组织经界内续行形成；教学电流在场（作用于输出 1）而不经实际链达 T
    o1 = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], [F(4, 3), F(0)], [F(1), F(1), F(0), F(0)], [F(0), F(0)])
    C1 = GK.Instance('C1：申报初态 v0 = 4/3、x0 = x1 = 1（初始组织，非迹应）；史 (静＋教学(0,1), 静, 静)', G,
                     [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o1)
    ch0, chaux, info = chains(C1)
    rows = classify(C1, ch0, chaux, o1)
    print(f'  【对照 C1】{C1.name}；T = {{w00}}')
    for r in rows:
        e = r['e']
        print(f"    {ename(e)} | c = {fr(e['c'])} | Ch_0 {int(r['c0'])} Ch_aux {int(r['ca'])} | SigDep_0 {len(r['sd0'])} | {r['cls']}")
    zend = C1.pre[len(C1.hist)][0][0][0] - G.b[0][0]
    rv = r_verdict(rows)
    print(f'    窗末 z(w00) = {fr(zend)} > ε：q = 1；r_R({{w00}}) = {rv[0]}（{rv[1]}：作用于 w00 的唯一有效事件无入场链）；TraceSet = 0')
    C1b = GK.Instance('', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 1), ('w', 1, 1)], o0=o1)
    ch0b, chauxb, _ = chains(C1b)
    rowsb = classify(C1b, ch0b, chauxb, o1)
    print(f'    同史 T′ = {{w01, w11}}（教学电流驱动输出 1 发放而写入）：分类 {[(ename(r["e"]), r["cls"]) for r in rowsb]}；'
          f'r_R(T′) = {r_verdict(rowsb)[0]}（申报初态 v0 = 4/3、x0 = x1 = 1 不是静息态，P4R 不成立，不归 S*₀；属弱读法 S 类）')
    print('    读法：教学电流在场不自动给出 r；r 的条件正判须「静息介质上入场分量经实际链写入 T、撤除改变该写入」（本程序为严格子域分类；现行判据见附录 A.14）。')
    print()
    return P, N, out

# ---------------- 三：TraceSet 与 Fld 判词路径 ----------------
def section_fld(G, P, N, out):
    import decision as DC
    print('三、TraceSet 与 Fld 判词路径（模型模式，A 栏）')
    res = {}
    for nm in ('实例 P', '实例 N'):
        I, rows, rv = out[nm]
        end = I.pre[len(I.hist)]
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        # T_max（〔三〕288）：窗末 z > ε 且有形成记录的结构位；形成按 D16 的逐分项越阈在全 X 上重求
        Iall = GK.Instance('', G, I.hist, I.t0, T=X)
        Tmax = [k for k in X if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and Iall.form[k]]
        q = {k: (GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and bool(I.form[k])) for k in I.T}
        t0 = time.time()
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        j = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        jn = DC.judge(I, D, False)
        trace = '1[严格]' if (all(q.values()) and str(rv[0]).startswith('1[')) else (0 if not all(q.values()) or rv[0] == 0 else '⊥')
        Q = GK.k_and(1 if trace == '1[严格]' else trace, j['Part'], j['HDyn'])
        Q = '1[严格]' if Q == 1 else Q
        print(f'  【{nm}】T = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in I.T)}}}：q = ' + '，'.join(f'{wname(k)} {int(v)}（窗末 z = {fr(GK.zk(G, end, k))}，形成拍 {I.form[k][:1]}）' for k, v in q.items()))
        print(f'    r = {rv[0]}（引理 {rv[1]}；不申报 严格子域对应前件 时 ⊥_src）；Ident = 1（POS：结构位即突触，位置持存）；HistEv = 1（REC：自申报初态全程记录）；TraceSet = {trace}')
        print(f'    Part = {j["Part"]}，HDyn = {j["HDyn"]}（定理 D2，申报 B_FD；不申报 B_FD：Part = {jn["Part"]}，HDyn = {jn["HDyn"]}）；Q_R(T) = {Q}')
        print(f'    T_max = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in Tmax)}}}')
        if set(Tmax) == set(I.T):
            QT = Q; jT = j
        else:
            IT = GK.Instance(I.name, G, I.hist, I.t0, T=Tmax)
            ch0, chaux, _ = chains(IT)
            rowsT = classify(IT, ch0, chaux, G.init())
            rT = r_verdict(rowsT)[0]
            qT = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and IT.form[k] for k in Tmax)
            DT = DC.Decider(IT)
            jT = DC.judge(IT, DT, True)
            jTn = DC.judge(IT, DT, False)
            trT = '1[严格]' if (qT and str(rT).startswith('1[')) else '⊥'
            QT = GK.k_and(1 if trT == '1[严格]' else trT, jT['Part'], jT['HDyn'])
            print(f'    在 T_max 上：r = {rT}，TraceSet = {trT}，Part = {jT["Part"]}，HDyn = {jT["HDyn"]}（不申报 B_FD：HDyn = {jTn["HDyn"]}）；Q_R(T_max) = {QT}')
            if jT['HDyn'] == 0:
                print('    HDyn(T_max) = 0 的前件：' + '；'.join(DC.premises(IT, DT, jT, True)))
        fld = '1[严格]' if (QT == 1 or Q == '1[严格]') else (0 if QT == 0 else '⊥')
        prem = '条件判 1：严格子域对应前件 成立时见证 T 上 Q_R = 1（〔三〕4.7「见证存在即足」）；不申报 严格子域对应前件 时 r = ⊥_src，TraceSet、Q_R、Fld = ⊥' if fld == '1[严格]' else \
            '判 0：Q_R(T_max) = 0，另需 EF1′（辅助位不承担迹应）、T_max 外由 EF1′ 承担，以及 HDyn 判 0 的前件清单（定理 D2）、r 不参与（TraceSet 已为 1）'
        print(f'    Fld = {fld}（{prem}）')
        res[nm] = (q, rv, trace, j, Q, Tmax, QT, fld)
    print()
    return res

# ---------------- 四：R3σ 与 S* 前件的独立重跑核验 ----------------
def entry_slot_reach(G, hist, u, comps, plastic=True, bap=True, noW=False):
    """槽位超图上自拍 u 的入场分量集 comps 的前向可达：返回 (本拍受影响的事件种类判定函数, R[t] 结点集)。"""
    name, I = hist[u]
    s = G.pat[name]
    ins = {i for (k, i) in comps if k == 'in'}
    js = {j for (k, j) in comps if k == 'I'}
    gates = set(js) | ({j for j in range(G.n_out)} if ins else set())   # 入口经 SYN 槽影响一切输出的门控
    nxt = set()
    for j in gates:
        nxt |= {('v', j), ('y', j)} | {('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in)}
    for i in ins:
        nxt |= {('x', i)} | {('w', i, j) for j in range(G.n_out)} | {('v', j) for j in range(G.n_out)}
    def affected_now(e):
        kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
        if kind in ('XIN',):
            return idx[0] in ins
        if kind == 'SYN':
            return idx[0] in ins
        if kind == 'INJ':
            return idx[0] in js
        if kind in ('RESET', 'YIN'):
            return idx[0] in gates
        if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP'):
            return idx[1] in gates or idx[0] in ins
        return False
    if noW:
        nxt = {n for n in nxt if n[0] != 'w'}
    R = {u + 1: frozenset(nxt)}
    cur = set(nxt)
    for t in range(u + 1, len(hist)):
        dep = RW.slot_deps(G, hist[t], plastic, bap)
        cur = {n for n, d in dep.items() if d & cur}
        if noW:
            cur = {n for n in cur if n[0] != 'w'}
        R[t + 1] = frozenset(cur)
    return affected_now, R

def random_gate(rng):
    n_in, n_out = 3, 2
    pat = {'none': (0, 0, 0), 'a': (1, 0, 0), 'b': (0, 1, 1), 'ab': (1, 1, 1)}
    lam = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for _ in range(n_out)]
    th = [F(1)] * n_out
    b = [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
    lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
    hi = [[F(rng.choice([1, 2])) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
    return GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, lam, th, [F(0)] * n_out, [F(1, 2)] * n_in, [F(1, 2)] * n_out,
                   F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8), F(rng.randint(0, 2), 16), F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1]), 10), b, lo, hi)

def random_hist(rng, G, L):
    letters = list(G.pat)
    h = []
    for _ in range(L):
        a = rng.choice(letters)
        I = tuple(F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2, -1, 3]), 4) for _ in range(G.n_out))
        h.append((a, I))
    return h

def section_checks():
    print('四、有限核验')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_cmp = n_viol = 0
    n_inst = 0
    # R3σ：槽位超图上自撤除分量不可达的事件，在撤除后不变
    while n_inst < 150:
        G = random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 8)
        h = random_hist(rng, G, L)
        o0 = G.init()
        pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, o0, h)
        for u in range(L):
            for sig in removals(G, h[u]):
                gone = set(entry_comps(G, h[u])) - set(entry_comps(G, sig))
                # 撤除使某分量改值（含负教学电流的撤除）：取「改了值」的分量集
                name, I = h[u]
                changed = {('I', j) for j in range(G.n_out) if I[j] != sig[1][j]} | \
                          {('in', i) for i in range(G.n_in) if G.pat[name][i] != G.pat[sig[0]][i]}
                aff, R = entry_slot_reach(G, h, u, changed)
                pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(G, pre[u], [sig] + h[u + 1:], t_start=u)
                for t in range(u, L):
                    for e in evs[t].values():
                        reach = aff(e) if t == u else bool(e['reads'] & R[t])
                        if not reach:
                            n_cmp += 1
                            if not GK.same_event(evs2, e):
                                n_viol += 1
        n_inst += 1
    print(f'  R3σ（入场不干涉）：150 个随机 K_g 成员（含负教学电流、弛豫），槽位不可达事件比较 {n_cmp} 次，撤除后改变者 {n_viol}')
    # S* 前件的独立核验：在随机史上，S* 类事件的撤除见证经完整重跑复核；S* 类事件在 Ch_0 上的链两端由事件读取集与字母独立复算
    rng = random.Random(7)
    tot = {'S*₀': 0, 'S*₁': 0, '⊥_aux': 0, 'S': 0, '⊥_σOpen': 0, '⊥_σOver': 0, '续行': 0, '无链': 0}
    rerun_fail = 0
    open_neg = 0
    for _ in range(120):
        G = random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 7)
        h = random_hist(rng, G, L)
        T = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=T)
        ch0, chaux, _ = chains(I)
        rows = classify(I, ch0, chaux, G.init(), lookback=3, tmax=L - 1)
        for r in rows:
            tot[r['cls']] += 1
            if r['cls'] == 'S*₀':
                sig, _w = r['sd0'][0]
                t = r['e']['t']
                pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(G, I.pre[t], [sig], t_start=t)
                if GK.same_event(evs2, r['e']):
                    rerun_fail += 1
            if r['cls'] == '⊥_σOpen':
                if any(F(x) < 0 for x in I.hist[r['e']['t']][1]):
                    open_neg += 1
    print(f'  来源分类（120 个随机成员，T = 全部突触，有效 T 事件）：' + '；'.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in tot.items()) +
          f'；S*₀ 撤除见证完整重跑复核失败 {rerun_fail}；⊥_σOpen 中本拍含负教学电流者 {open_neg}')
    print('  读法：「续行」= 撤除较早入场改变该事件，但在去掉结构位的槽位超图上不可达——依赖只经已留迹应传递（全为读本位的弛豫），属场内续行，不是入场依赖；')
    print('        ⊥_σOpen = 撤除依赖可经非结构位传递而实际链不存在（依赖只经未发生的发放，如撤除抑制性负电流）；按负向第（c）档保持 ⊥，不签 1。')
    print()

# ---------------- 五：与 D16（F 层 Src/Mark）的对照 ----------------
class FM:
    """F 类机制模型（第三篇 A.4 MTM 的最小子集）：结构位、界外脉冲（按字母）、机制（常数/线性律，载体 B/界外，可按字母门控）。"""
    def __init__(self, sites, s0, T, eps, pulses, mechs):
        self.sites, self.s0, self.T, self.eps = sites, dict(zip(sites, s0)), T, eps
        self.pulses, self.mechs = pulses, mechs          # pulses: {字母: [(位, 量)]}；mech: dict(name,tgt,car,law,reads,k,b,off,beats)
    def run(self, hist):
        s = dict(self.s0)
        ev_all = []
        for t, u in enumerate(hist):
            evs = []
            for (tg, a) in self.pulses.get(u, []):
                if a > self.eps:
                    evs.append(dict(kind='pulse', name=f'p{tg}', tgt=tg, c=F(a), car='界外', letter=True, reads=()))
            for m in self.mechs:
                if m.get('beats') is not None and t not in m['beats']:
                    continue
                if u in m.get('off', ()):
                    continue
                c = m['b'] + (m['k'] * sum(s[r] for r in m['reads']) if m['law'] == 'lin' else 0)
                gated = bool(m.get('off')) and 'rest' in m.get('off', ())     # 静置下不运行 ⇒ 发生由入场字母定
                evs.append(dict(kind='mech', name=m['name'], tgt=m['tgt'], c=c, car=m['car'], letter=gated, reads=m['reads']))
            ev_all.append((t, u, dict(s), evs))
            for e in evs:
                if abs(e['c']) > self.eps:
                    s[e['tgt']] += e['c']
        return ev_all, s

def d16_mark(M, hist):
    ev_all, _ = M.run(hist)
    mark = {i: [] for i in M.T}
    for t, u, s, evs in ev_all:
        for e in evs:
            if e['tgt'] in M.T and abs(e['c']) > M.eps and e['car'] == '界外':
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name']))
    return mark

def lemmaS_mark(M, hist):
    """F 模型上的引理 S 判读：条款 0（载体界外或界外脉冲的有效改变，即信号本身的作用）；
    条款 1（S*）：静置下不运行的字母驱动 B 内写入，读取集为空，拍前为申报初态（F 模型对照中的 P4 形式；F 位皆为结构位，无辅助态），撤除入场（字母换静置）则不发生，静置下 T 上无有效事件。
    F 位皆为结构位，链不能跨拍，故条款 1 只可能经字母门控边成立。"""
    ev_all, _ = M.run(hist)
    mark = {i: [] for i in M.T}
    for t, u, s, evs in ev_all:
        for e in evs:
            if e['tgt'] not in M.T or abs(e['c']) <= M.eps:
                continue
            if e['car'] == '界外':
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], '条款0'))
            elif e['letter'] and not e['reads'] and u != 'rest' and s == M.s0 and t == 0:
                # P5：本拍换静置后 T 上无有效事件
                h2 = list(hist); h2[t] = 'rest'
                ev2, _ = M.run(h2[:t + 1])
                if not any(x['tgt'] in M.T and abs(x['c']) > M.eps for x in ev2[t][3]):
                    mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], '条款1（S*）'))
    return mark

def section_d16():
    print('五、与 D16（F 层 Src/Mark）的对照')
    H = F(1, 2)
    seed = dict(name='seed', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), beats=(0,))
    reset_g = dict(name='reset', tgt='g', car='B', law='lin', reads=('g',), k=F(-1), b=F(0))
    A3 = FM(('x', 'g'), (0, 0), ('x',), F(0), {'a': [('g', 1)]},
            [seed, dict(name='decay', tgt='x', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=-H, b=F(0)),
             dict(name='amp', tgt='x', car='界外', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=H, b=F(0), off=('rest',)), reset_g])
    B3 = FM(('x', 'g'), (0, 0), ('x',), F(0), {'a': [('g', 1)]},
            [seed, dict(name='decay', tgt='x', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=-H, b=F(0), off=('a',)), reset_g])
    h3 = ['rest', 'a', 'rest']
    B5 = FM(('x',), (1,), ('x',), F(0), {},
            [dict(name='g', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), off=('rest',)),
             dict(name='e+', tgt='x', car='界外', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), beats=(1,)),
             dict(name='i-', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(-1), beats=(1,))])
    B5p = FM(('x',), (0,), ('x',), F(0), {},
             [dict(name='g', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), off=('rest',))])
    h5 = ['w', 'rest']
    for nm, M, h, claim in (('A3（界外载体放大 +x/2 与界内退浅相抵）', A3, h3, '第三篇：r = 1'),
                            ('B3（信号只停下界内退浅）', B3, h3, '第三篇：r = 0'),
                            ('B5（字母门控界内常数律＋界外/界内相抵对）', B5, h5, '第三篇：两方 r 均成立（以相抵对保证）'),
                            ('B5′（B5 去掉相抵对，初值 0）', B5p, h5, '第三篇未判')):
        m1, m2 = d16_mark(M, h), lemmaS_mark(M, h)
        s1 = int(any(m1[i] for i in M.T)); s2 = int(any(m2[i] for i in M.T))
        print(f'  {nm}：D16 Src_F = {s1}（Mark {m1["x"]}）；引理 S 的 r = {s2}（{m2["x"]}）；{claim}')
    # F^σ-free 子类：随机 F 模型（无字母门控的 B 内机制；界外机制与界外脉冲可任意），逐位比较 Mark 集合
    rng = random.Random(31)
    same = diff = 0
    for _ in range(600):
        sites = ('x', 'y', 'z')
        T = tuple(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, 3)))
        pulses = {a: [(rng.choice(sites), F(rng.randint(0, 3), 2))] for a in ('a', 'b') if rng.random() < 0.7}
        mechs = []
        for n in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
            car = rng.choice(['B', 'B', '界外'])
            law = rng.choice(['const', 'lin'])
            m = dict(name=f'm{n}', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car=car, law=law,
                     reads=(rng.choice(sites),) if law == 'lin' else (), k=F(rng.randint(-1, 2), 4), b=F(rng.randint(0, 2), 4))
            if car == '界外' and rng.random() < 0.5:
                m['off'] = ('rest',)
            mechs.append(m)
        M = FM(sites, (0, 0, 0), T, F(1, 100), pulses, mechs)
        h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a', 'b']) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
        try:
            m1, m2 = d16_mark(M, h), lemmaS_mark(M, h)
        except Exception:
            continue
        a = {i: sorted((t, n) for t, n in m1[i]) for i in T}
        b = {i: sorted((t, n) for t, n, _c in m2[i]) for i in T}
        same += (a == b); diff += (a != b)
    print(f'  F^σ-free 子类随机 600 例：引理 S 的 Mark 集合与 D16 逐位相同 {same} 例，不同 {diff} 例')
    rng = random.Random(32)
    dv = tot = 0
    kinds = set()
    for _ in range(600):
        sites = ('x', 'y')
        T = ('x',)
        mechs = [dict(name='g', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(rng.randint(1, 3), 4), off=('rest',))]
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            mechs.append(dict(name='m', tgt='x', car=rng.choice(['B', '界外']), law='lin', reads=('y',), k=F(rng.randint(-2, 2), 4), b=F(0)))
        M = FM(sites, (0, 0), T, F(1, 100), {}, mechs)
        h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))]
        m1, m2 = d16_mark(M, h), lemmaS_mark(M, h)
        tot += 1
        if bool(m1['x']) != bool(m2['x']):
            dv += 1
            kinds |= {c for (_t, _n, c) in m2['x'] if (_t, _n) not in m1['x']}
    print(f'  含字母门控 B 内写入的随机 600 例：Src 判值分歧 {dv} 例；分歧全部来自 {sorted(kinds)}（即「字母驱动、载体在 B 内的写入」，接口缺口 G-r2 的位置）')
    print()

def section_grid(S):
    """冻结 SNN 参数网格 108 组（snn_scan.py 的申报史）上 q、r、TraceSet 的逐组求值；Part、HDyn 取 lp03_exact.py 运行记录（附录 B.2 第二项）的分组结果。"""
    print('六、冻结 SNN 参数网格 108 组上的 q、r 与 TraceSet（T = {w00, w10}；申报史：形成 (A+教学, 静, 静)×n，静置 8，W 为 (A,静,静,B,静,静)×3）')
    W0s, APs, Ts, Ns = [F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)], [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]
    tab = {}
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product(W0s, APs, Ts, Ns):
        S.W0, S.A_PLUS = W0, AP
        G = GC.snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * 2) * n
        hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
        I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, len(form) + 8)
        ch0, chaux, _ = chains(I)
        rows = classify(I, ch0, chaux, G.init())
        rv = r_verdict(rows)[0]
        end = I.pre[len(hist)]
        q = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and I.form[k] for k in I.T)
        tr = '1[严格]' if (q and str(rv).startswith('1[')) else (0 if (not q or rv == 0) else '⊥')
        key = (int(q), str(rv) + '／' + r_verdict(rows)[1], str(tr))
        tab.setdefault(key, []).append((W0, AP, teach, n))
    S.W0, S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
    for (q, rv, tr), lst in sorted(tab.items()):
        ex = lst[0]
        print(f'  q = {q}，r = {rv}，TraceSet = {tr}：{len(lst)} 组（例 W0 = {ex[0]}、A₊ = {ex[1]}、教学 {ex[2]}、n = {ex[3]}）')
    nq0r = len([1 for (q, rv, tr), lst in tab.items() for _ in lst if q == 0])
    print(f'  读法：q = 0 的组 TraceSet = 0，与 r 无关；q = 1 的 70 组 r 全部为「严格子域对应前件 下 1」：多数由 S*₀（形成拍 0，拍前为静息态），其余由 S*₁（史首静息、首次发放出现在第二、三次配对，链经膜电位跨拍，另需 EF1′）。不申报 严格子域对应前件 时这 70 组 r = ⊥_src。')
    print('  与核心任务 run_lp03_exact.txt 的分组对照：q = 1 的 70 组中 Part = 1；HDyn = 1 者 48 组（原「成立」27、「FP」12、「P」3、W0 = 3/8 的「PH」3、W0 = 1/2 且 A₊ = 1/4、n = 6 的「FPH」3）——'
          '这 48 组在 严格子域对应前件 下 Q_R(T) = 1、Fld = 1（不申报 严格子域对应前件 时 ⊥）；其余 q = 1 的 22 组只得所列 T 上 Q_R(T) = 0（HDyn = 0：7 组第（a）档、15 组第（b）档须 B_FD，不申报 B_FD 时 ⊥_Over），Fld 待 T_max，不读作 0。')
    print()

def section_aux(S):
    print('七、预置辅助态的回归：静置不动点不排除非零辅助记忆；端点 λ、d_x、d_y 取 1')
    H = F(1, 2)
    def gate1(lam, dx, dy):
        return GC.Gate(1, 1, {'none': (0,), 'A': (1,)}, [lam], [F(1)], [F(0)], [dx], [dy], F(1, 8), F(0), F(0),
                       [[F(1, 4)]], [[F(0)]], [[F(2)]])
    G = gate1(F(0), F(1), H)
    o = ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)])
    I = GK.Instance('预置资格迹例', G, [('none', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o)
    o0 = ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0)])
    I0 = GK.Instance('', G, [('none', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o0)
    c1 = I.evs[0].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 0)); c0 = I0.evs[0].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 0))
    rows = classify(I, *chains(I)[:2], o)
    print(f'  预置资格迹例（n_in = n_out = 1，λ = 0，d_x = 1，d_y = 1/2，A₊ = 1/8，A₋ = 0，ρ = 0，b = 1/4；状态 w = b、v = 0、x = 1、y = 0）：')
    print(f'    rest_state（P4R）= {rest_state(I, o)}；P4Z = {zero_aux(I, o)}；字母 none＋I = 1：x = 1 时 PLUS = {fr(c1["c"]) if c1 else 0}，x = 0 时 PLUS = {fr(c0["c"]) if c0 else 0}')
    print(f'    严格子域分类：{[(ename(r["e"]), r["cls"]) for r in rows]}；r_R = {r_verdict(rows)[0]}（不判 0）')
    rows0 = classify(I0, *chains(I0)[:2], o0)
    print(f'    同参数、辅助态为 0：分类 {[(ename(r["e"]), r["cls"]) for r in rows0]}（PLUS 分项为 0，无有效写入；无入口刺激时教学电流单独不留迹应）')
    print('  端点表（每行：λ、d_x、d_y；非零静止辅助态是否存在；该态上 P4R、P4Z；零辅助态上 SF 正例的分类）：')
    for lam, dx, dy in [(F(1), H, H), (H, F(1), H), (H, H, F(1)), (F(1), F(1), F(1)), (H, H, H)]:
        Gx = gate1(lam, dx, dy)
        cand = []
        if lam == 1: cand.append(('v', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(1, 2)], [F(0)], [F(0)])))
        if dx == 1: cand.append(('x', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)])))
        if dy == 1: cand.append(('y', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(1)])))
        res = []
        for nm, oo in cand:
            Ix = GK.Instance('', Gx, [('A', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=oo)
            rx = classify(Ix, *chains(Ix)[:2], oo)
            res.append(f'{nm}≠0：P4R {rest_state(Ix, oo)}、P4Z {zero_aux(Ix, oo)}、分类 {sorted({r["cls"] for r in rx})}')
        oz = Gx.init()
        Iz = GK.Instance('', Gx, [('A', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=oz)
        rz = classify(Iz, *chains(Iz)[:2], oz)
        print(f'    λ = {lam}、d_x = {dx}、d_y = {dy}：' + ('；'.join(res) if res else '无非零静止辅助态（λ、d < 1 时静置不动点的辅助位必为 0）') +
              f'；零辅助态、字母 A＋I = 1：分类 {sorted({r["cls"] for r in rz})}')
    print('  读法：非零静止辅助态只在对应系数取 1 时出现，在该态上 P4R 成立而 P4Z 不成立，事件归 ⊥_aux（来源子域外），不归 S*₀；零辅助态上 S*₀ 照常。')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = GK.Instance('', G, FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    print(f'  冻结 SNN：λ = {G.lam[0]}、d_x = {G.dx[0]}、d_y = {G.dy[0]}（均 < 1）；拍 0 拍前态 P4R = {rest_state(P, P.pre[0])}、P4Z = {zero_aux(P, P.pre[0])}——在严格子域内，实例 P 的 S*₀ 见证不变（第二节）。')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h}（与冻结值相符）')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    P, N, out = section_snn(G)
    section_fld(G, P, N, out)
    section_checks()
    section_d16()
    section_grid(S)
    section_aux(S)
    print('结束。以上为有限核验与精确读数；一般命题的证明见正文第五节。')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.14　source\_r4.py　判据 PS、引理 CH₀ 与 CH、定理 SUB 与 STR、过定、端点例、D16 对照、网格来源（4.3、5.4、5.8、7.5）

~~~python
"""参与型来源认证判据 PS（正文 4.3、5.4）：实例 P、N、C1 的逐写入判定；TraceSet 与 Fld；预置辅助态端点例的逐例判定；过定例；引理 CH₀、CH，定理 SUB、STR 与过定族的有限核验；D16 的 F 模型对照；网格的 q、r、TraceSet。
判据：链 Ch^D15（实际事件、函数支持）；容许撤除（撤除域任意分量子集，无开启条件 g′ ≤ g，跨拍另加迹应钳定）；M1₀（同拍）、M1_aux（跨拍，须 EF1′）、M2（门控过定的 NESS 检验，须 B_OD）；原因码 ⊥_OD、⊥_σOpen、⊥_σOver；无链、续行。
输出中「严格子域」列是附录 A.13 的分类，「PS」列是本程序的判定。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 source_r4.py
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import source_r3 as S3
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr
ename, wname = S3.ename, S3.wname
removals, changed_comps, entry_comps = S3.removals, S3.changed_comps, S3.entry_comps
rest_state, zero_aux, null_letter = S3.rest_state, S3.zero_aux, S3.null_letter

def cname(c):
    return f"{'入口' if c[0] == 'in' else '教学电流 I'}{c[1]}@{c[2]}"

# ---------------- 定义 S-2′：实际作用链 Ch^D15 ----------------
def tick_chain4(G, o, sigma, evs, carried, t):
    """返回 (R, gate, carried_next, why)。R：事件键 (kind, idx) → 链起点集（入场分量 (种类, 下标, 拍)）。
    边：('in', i) → XIN_i、SYN_ij（c = w_ij ≠ 0 时才汇入门控）、MINUS_ij（发生）、PLUS_ij（分项经 x′_i）；('I', j) → INJ_j → γ_j；
        携带的 v_j 在 λ_j ≠ 0 时汇入 γ_j（D15：vt 对 v 的偏导为 λ_j）；γ_j（只在 g_j = 1 时存在）→ RESET_j、YIN_j、PLUS_ij（发生）、MINUS_ij（经 y′_j）；
        携带的 x_i 在 d_x,i ≠ 0 时进入 PLUS_ij 的分项，携带的 y_j 在 d_y,j ≠ 0 时进入 MINUS_ij 的分项；CLIP 经其在链上的成员。
    跨拍：v_j 复位时取 γ_j 的起点集，否则取本拍汇入（SYN、INJ）之并，λ_j ≠ 0 时并入原携带；x_i、y_j 同理（d ≠ 0 时保留原携带）。
    链不经结构位 w 的结点。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    ev = {(e['id'][0], e['id'][1]): e for e in evs.values()}
    R, why = {}, {}
    def add(key, reason, orig):
        if key in ev and orig:
            if key not in R:
                why[key] = reason
            R.setdefault(key, set()).update(orig)
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        if s[i]:
            add(('XIN', (i,)), f'入口{i}', {('in', i, t)})
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                add(('SYN', (i, j)), f'入口{i}经突触 w{i}{j} 传递', {('in', i, t)})
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if I[j] != 0:
            add(('INJ', (j,)), f'教学电流 I{j}', {('I', j, t)})
    gate, gsrc = {}, {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:                                   # g_j = 1
            orig, src = set(), []
            for i in range(G.n_in):
                k = ('SYN', (i, j))
                if k in R and ev[k]['c'] != 0:
                    orig |= R[k]; src.append(f'SYN{i}{j}（{fr(ev[k]["c"])}）')
            if ('INJ', (j,)) in R:
                orig |= R[('INJ', (j,))]; src.append(f'INJ{j}（{fr(ev[("INJ", (j,))]["c"])}）')
            if G.lam[j] != 0 and carried.get(('v', j)):
                orig |= carried[('v', j)]; src.append(f'携带的 v{j}（λ·v = {fr(G.lam[j] * v[j])}）')
            if orig:
                gate[j] = orig; gsrc[j] = src
    for j in gate:
        add(('RESET', (j,)), f'发放 γ{j}', gate[j]); add(('YIN', (j,)), f'发放 γ{j}', gate[j])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] != 0 else set())
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            key = ('PLUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                rs = []
                if j in gate: rs.append(f'发放 γ{j} 经反传到达突触（发生；γ{j} 的汇入：' + '、'.join(gsrc[j]) + '）')
                if xo: rs.append(f'x′{i} 携入场（分项）')
                add(key, '；'.join(rs), gate.get(j, set()) | xo)
            key = ('MINUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                yo = gate.get(j, set()) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] != 0 else set())
                add(key, f'入口{i}（发生）' + (f'；y′{j} 携入场（分项）' if yo else ''), {('in', i, t)} | yo)
            key = ('CLIP', (i, j))
            mem = [m for m in (('PLUS', (i, j)), ('MINUS', (i, j))) if m in R]
            if key in ev and mem:
                add(key, '夹限成员在链上', set().union(*[R[m] for m in mem]))
    C2 = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:
            if j in gate:
                C2[('v', j)] = set(gate[j])
        else:
            cs = set()
            for k in [('INJ', (j,))] + [('SYN', (i, j)) for i in range(G.n_in)]:
                if k in R and ev[k]['c'] != 0:
                    cs |= R[k]
            if G.lam[j] != 0 and carried.get(('v', j)):
                cs |= carried[('v', j)]
            if cs:
                C2[('v', j)] = cs
        yo = set(gate.get(j, set())) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] != 0 else set())
        if yo:
            C2[('y', j)] = yo
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] != 0 else set())
        if xo:
            C2[('x', i)] = xo
    return R, gate, C2, why

def chains4(I):
    ch0, chaux, why = {}, {}, {}
    carried = {}
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        R0, _, _, w0 = tick_chain4(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], {}, t)
        Ra, _, carried, wa = tick_chain4(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], carried, t)
        ch0[t], chaux[t], why[t] = R0, Ra, (w0, wa)
    return ch0, chaux, why

# ---------------- 容许撤除与认证方法 ----------------
def gates_at(G, o, sig, kw):
    return G.step(o, sig, **kw)[1]['g']

def no_activation(G, o, sig, sig2, kw):
    """无开启条件：撤除后各门控不大于实际门控（不使任何实际未开启的门控开启）。"""
    g1, g2 = gates_at(G, o, sig, kw), gates_at(G, o, sig2, kw)
    return all(b <= a for a, b in zip(g1, g2))

def event_change(evs2, e):
    t, eid = e['t'], e['id']
    if eid not in evs2:
        return '不发生'
    if evs2[eid]['c'] != e['c']:
        return f"分项 {fr(e['c'])}→{fr(evs2[eid]['c'])}"
    return None

def m1_0(I, e):
    """同拍容许撤除见证：[(σ′, 变化, 所撤分量集)]；另返回改变 e 而不容许（开启门控）的撤除。"""
    t = e['t']
    ok, bad = [], []
    for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[t]):
        evs2 = RW.Ev(I.G, I.pre[t], sig, t, **I.kw)
        ch = event_change(evs2, e)
        if ch is None:
            continue
        S = changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[t], sig, t)
        (ok if no_activation(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], sig, I.kw) else bad).append((sig, ch, S))
    return ok, bad

def clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, t):
    """拍 u 撤除 sig，自 u 重跑到 t；u 之后各拍的结构位钳定为实际值（迹应钳定）。返回 (evs2, 无开启是否成立)。"""
    def interv(tau, o):
        if tau > u:
            return (I.pre[tau][0], o[1], o[2], o[3])
        return o
    pre2, evs2, g2 = GK.run_with(I.G, I.pre[u], [sig] + I.hist[u + 1:t + 1], t_start=u, interv=interv, **I.kw)
    noact = all(all(b <= a for a, b in zip(I.gates[tau], g2[tau])) for tau in range(u, t + 1))
    return evs2, noact, g2

def m1_aux(I, e, lookback=6):
    t = e['t']
    for u in range(max(0, t - lookback), t):
        for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[u]):
            evs2, noact, _ = clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, t)
            if noact and not GK.same_event(evs2, e):
                return (u, sig, changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[u], sig, u), event_change(evs2[t], e))
    return None

def gate_summands(G, o, sig, j):
    """门控 j 的积分 vt_j 的实际分项：('dom', v_j) → λ_j v_j；('in', i) → w_ij（s_i = 1）；('I', j) → I_j。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    out = [(('dom', 'v', j), G.lam[j] * v[j])]
    out += [(('in', i), w[i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]]
    if I[j] != 0:
        out.append((('I', j), F(I[j])))
    return out

def m2_ness(I, e):
    """M2（NESS）：e 的信号依赖经门控 γ_j（PLUS 的发生、MINUS 的分项）；存在含某一信号正贡献 c 的实际充分子集，去掉 c 即不足。
    充分性在全部负贡献同在的条件下判（N = 负贡献之和）：∃ A ⊆ 其余正贡献，Σ A + N < θ ≤ Σ A + N + c。"""
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    if kind not in ('PLUS', 'MINUS'):
        return None
    t = e['t']; j = idx[1]
    if not I.gates[t][j]:
        return None
    if kind == 'MINUS' and I.G.Am == 0:
        return None
    sm = gate_summands(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], j)
    N = sum((c for _, c in sm if c < 0), F(0))
    pos = [(tag, c) for tag, c in sm if c > 0]
    th = I.G.theta[j]
    for k, (tag, c) in enumerate(pos):
        if tag[0] == 'dom':
            continue
        others = pos[:k] + pos[k + 1:]
        for r in range(len(others) + 1):
            for A in itertools.combinations(others, r):
                sA = sum((a for _, a in A), F(0))
                if sA + N < th <= sA + N + c:
                    return (tag, c, [a for a, _ in A], N)
    return None

def gate_overdetermined(I, e):
    """PLUS/MINUS 的门控 γ_j 在全部同拍容许撤除下仍开启（门控由界内贡献过定）。"""
    t = e['t']; j = e['id'][1][1]
    if not I.gates[t][j]:
        return False
    for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[t]):
        if no_activation(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], sig, I.kw) and not gates_at(I.G, I.pre[t], sig, I.kw)[j]:
            return False
    return True

def coparticipants(I, e):
    """与信号共同参与 e 的界内条件（逐项列出读数非零者）：已留迹应、辅助位、基线。"""
    G, o = I.G, I.pre[e['t']]
    w, v, x, y = o
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    out = []
    if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP'):
        i, j = idx
        if G.lam[j] * v[j] != 0:
            out.append(f'v{j} = {fr(v[j])}（经门控）')
        sig = I.hist[e['t']]
        trace = [f'w{a}{j} 迹应 {fr(w[a][j] - G.b[a][j])}' for a in range(G.n_in) if G.pat[sig[0]][a] and abs(w[a][j] - G.b[a][j]) > EPS]
        if trace:
            out.append('已留迹应经门控：' + '、'.join(trace))
        if kind == 'PLUS' and G.dx[i] * x[i] != 0:
            out.append(f'x{i} = {fr(x[i])}（分项）')
        if kind == 'MINUS' and G.dy[j] * y[j] != 0:
            out.append(f'y{j} = {fr(y[j])}（分项）')
    return out or ['无（辅助位为 0、门控读到的突触无迹应）']

def classify4(I, lookback=6, tmax=None, old=True, aux=True):
    ch0, chaux, why = chains4(I)
    rows = []
    tmax = I.t1 if tmax is None else tmax
    oldcls = {}
    if old:
        c0, ca, _ = S3.chains(I)
        for r in S3.classify(I, c0, ca, I.pre[0], lookback=lookback, tmax=tmax):
            oldcls[r['e']['id']] = r['cls']
    for t in range(0, tmax + 1):
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] not in I.T or abs(e['c']) <= EPS:
                continue
            key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
            o0 = ch0[t].get(key, set()); oa = chaux[t].get(key, set())
            ok, bad = m1_0(I, e)
            wit = None
            if ok:
                cls, wit = 'M1₀', ok
            else:
                ma = m1_aux(I, e, lookback) if (aux and oa) else None
                if ma:
                    cls, wit = 'M1_aux', ma
                else:
                    m2 = m2_ness(I, e) if oa else None
                    if m2:
                        cls, wit = 'M2', m2
                    elif oa:
                        if e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') and gate_overdetermined(I, e):
                            cls = '⊥_OD'
                        elif bad:
                            cls = '⊥_σOpen'
                        else:
                            cls = '⊥_σOver'
                    else:
                        w0 = S3.sigdep0(I, e)
                        wm = None if w0 else S3.sigdep_m(I, e, lookback)
                        if w0 or wm:
                            w_ = (t, w0[0][0]) if w0 else wm
                            cls = '⊥_σOpen' if S3.wfree_reach(I, e, w_) else '续行'
                        else:
                            cls = '无链'
            p4r = rest_state(I, I.pre[t])
            rows.append(dict(e=e, cls=cls, wit=wit, o0=o0, oa=oa, why=why[t][1].get(key, '—'),
                             p4r=p4r, p4z=zero_aux(I, I.pre[t]), old=oldcls.get(e['id'])))
    return rows

def r_verdict4(rows):
    cl = [r['cls'] for r in rows]
    if 'M1₀' in cl:
        return '1', 'M1₀'
    if 'M1_aux' in cl:
        return '1[EF1′]', 'M1_aux'
    if 'M2' in cl:
        return '1[B_OD]', 'M2'
    if all(c in ('无链', '续行') for c in cl):
        return 0, 'S⁻（读法 N）'
    return '⊥', '/'.join(sorted({c for c in cl if c.startswith('⊥')}))

def count(rows, key='cls'):
    cnt = {}
    for r in rows:
        cnt[r[key]] = cnt.get(r[key], 0) + 1
    return '；'.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(cnt.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])))

def show_wit(r):
    c = r['cls']
    w = r['wit']
    if c == 'M1₀':
        sig, ch, S = w[0]
        return f'撤除至 {sig[0]}＋I=({",".join(fr(F(a)) for a in sig[1])})（所撤 {"、".join(cname(x) for x in sorted(S))}）：{ch}'
    if c == 'M1_aux':
        u, sig, S, ch = w
        return f'拍 {u} 撤除至 {sig[0]}＋I=({",".join(fr(F(a)) for a in sig[1])})（所撤 {"、".join(cname(x) for x in sorted(S))}），迹应钳定重跑：{ch}'
    if c == 'M2':
        tag, cc, A, N = w
        return f'NESS：信号贡献 {tag}={fr(cc)}，充分子集另含 {A or "∅"}（负贡献和 {fr(N)}）'
    return '—'

# ---------------- 一、二：SNN 实例 ----------------
def section_snn(G):
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                    [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    print('一、冻结 SNN 实例 P 的来源重证（参与型判据 PS；作用于 T = {w00, w10} 的有效事件逐一）')
    rows = classify4(P)
    print(f'  有效事件 {len(rows)} 个；PS分类：{count(rows)}；严格子域分类：{count(rows, "old")}')
    shown = set()
    for r in rows:
        e = r['e']
        tag = (e['id'][0], P.hist[e['t']][0], P.hist[e['t']][1] != (0, 0))
        if e['t'] != 0 and tag in shown:
            continue
        shown.add(tag)
        print(f"    {ename(e)} | c = {fr(e['c'])} | 字母 {P.hist[e['t']][0]}＋I=({','.join(fr(F(a)) for a in P.hist[e['t']][1])}) | "
              f"P4R {int(r['p4r'])} P4Z {int(r['p4z'])} | 严格子域 {r['old']} → PS {r['cls']}")
        print(f"        链：{r['why']}；起点 {sorted(cname(c) for c in r['o0'])}")
        print(f"        来源承担：{show_wit(r)}；共同参与：{'；'.join(coparticipants(P, e))}")
    print('  全部 36 个逐一（事件｜严格子域 → PS｜同拍容许撤除见证）：')
    for r in rows:
        print(f"    {ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])}｜{r['old']} → {r['cls']}｜{show_wit(r)}")
    rv = r_verdict4(rows)
    print(f'  r_R(T) = {rv[0]}（依据 {rv[1]}；不耗 P4R、P4Z、EF1′；M1₀ 只用同拍撤除）')
    n_in_only = 0
    for r in rows:
        e = r['e']; t = e['t']
        for sig in removals(G, P.hist[t]):
            if tuple(sig[1]) != tuple(P.hist[t][1]):
                continue
            if no_activation(G, P.pre[t], P.hist[t], sig, P.kw) and event_change(RW.Ev(G, P.pre[t], sig, t, **P.kw), e):
                n_in_only += 1
                break
    print(f'  边界另立实例的核对（教学电流装置划入 B，撤除域只含入口分量）：36 个写入中由「只撤入口、教学电流保持」的容许撤除认证者 {n_in_only} 个'
          '（只核撤除见证；该实例的其余申报未另立，教学电流划入 B 后其载体与事件个别化须重申报）')
    nM1 = sum(1 for r in rows if r['cls'] == 'M1₀')
    print(f'  作用于 T 的有效事件中 M1₀ 认证 {nM1}/{len(rows)}；拍 0 的 4 个（原 S*₀）与其后 32 个（原 S 类，入场与已留迹应、辅助位共同参与）同由 M1₀ 认证')
    out = {'P': (P, rows, rv)}
    rowsN = classify4(N)
    rvN = r_verdict4(rowsN)
    print(f'  【实例 N】{N.name}：有效事件 {len(rowsN)} 个，PS {count(rowsN)}（严格子域 {count(rowsN, "old")}）；r_R(T) = {rvN[0]}')
    out['N'] = (N, rowsN, rvN)
    o1 = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], [F(4, 3), F(0)], [F(1), F(1), F(0), F(0)], [F(0), F(0)])
    C1 = GK.Instance('C1', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o1)
    rowsC = classify4(C1)
    print(f'  【对照 C1】申报初态 v0 = 4/3、x0 = x1 = 1；史 (静＋教学(0,1), 静, 静)；T = {{w00}}：')
    for r in rowsC:
        print(f"    {ename(r['e'])} | c = {fr(r['e']['c'])} | 严格子域 {r['old']} → PS {r['cls']}（门控 γ0 由预置 v0 单独越阈：λ·v0 = {fr(G.lam[0] * F(4, 3))} ≥ θ；教学电流只作用于输出 1，无自入场分量到 w00 的实际链）")
    print(f'    r_R({{w00}}) = {r_verdict4(rowsC)[0]}（{r_verdict4(rowsC)[1]}；前件 REC、ENT、R1、EF1′ 与读法 N）；q = 1；TraceSet = 0')
    C1b = GK.Instance('C1′', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 1), ('w', 1, 1)], o0=o1)
    rowsB = classify4(C1b)
    print(f'  【对照 C1 的 T′ = {{w01, w11}}】')
    for r in rowsB:
        print(f"    {ename(r['e'])} | c = {fr(r['e']['c'])} | 严格子域 {r['old']}（P4R、P4Z 不成立）→ PS {r['cls']}；{show_wit(r)}；共同参与：{'；'.join(coparticipants(C1b, r['e']))}")
    print(f'    r_R(T′) = {r_verdict4(rowsB)[0]}（严格子域：RS 下 1，否则 ⊥_RS；PS：教学电流 I1 经 INJ1 → γ1 → PLUS 的实际链参与，撤除即不发生；预置 x0、x1 共同参与分项，不构成负判）')
    print()
    return P, N, out

def section_fld(G, out):
    import decision as DC
    print('二、TraceSet 与 Fld 判词路径（实例 P、N；模型模式，算法层，A 栏）')
    for nm in ('P', 'N'):
        I, rows, rv = out[nm]
        end = I.pre[len(I.hist)]
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        Iall = GK.Instance('', G, I.hist, I.t0, T=X)
        Tmax = [k for k in X if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and Iall.form[k]]
        q = {k: (GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and bool(I.form[k])) for k in I.T}
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        j, jn = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
        trace = 1 if (all(q.values()) and rv[0] == '1') else (0 if not all(q.values()) or rv[0] == 0 else '⊥')
        Q = GK.k_and(trace, j['Part'], j['HDyn'])
        print(f'  【实例 {nm}】T = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in I.T)}}}：q = ' + '，'.join(f'{wname(k)} {int(v)}（窗末 z = {fr(GK.zk(G, end, k))}）' for k, v in q.items()) +
              f'；r = {rv[0]}；Ident = 1（POS）；HistEv = 1（REC）；TraceSet = {trace}')
        print(f'    Part = {j["Part"]}，HDyn = {j["HDyn"]}（不申报 B_FD：{jn["Part"]}、{jn["HDyn"]}）；Q_R(T) = {Q}；T_max = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in Tmax)}}}')
        if set(Tmax) != set(I.T):
            IT = GK.Instance(I.name, G, I.hist, I.t0, T=Tmax)
            rT = r_verdict4(classify4(IT, old=False))[0]
            DT = DC.Decider(IT)
            jT = DC.judge(IT, DT, True)
            qT = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and IT.form[k] for k in Tmax)
            QT = GK.k_and(1 if (qT and rT == '1') else '⊥', jT['Part'], jT['HDyn'])
            print(f'    在 T_max 上：r = {rT}，Part = {jT["Part"]}，HDyn = {jT["HDyn"]}；Q_R(T_max) = {QT}')
        else:
            QT = Q
        fld = 1 if Q == 1 else (0 if QT == 0 else '⊥')
        print(f'    Fld = {fld}' + ('（算法层；见证 T 上 Q_R = 1，回读由定理 R7；r 由 M1₀；其余前件见证书前件表_严格子域）' if fld == 1 else
                                   '（Q_R(T_max) = 0；HDyn 第（a）档，前件 MOD-R、R2/R3、REC、ENT、POS、BEAT-R 与 EF1′；不耗 B_FD）'))
    print()

# ---------------- 三：原 ⊥_aux 情形与端点回归（逐例） ----------------
def gate1(lam, dx, dy, Am=F(0), b=F(1, 4), vreset=F(0)):
    return GC.Gate(1, 1, {'none': (0,), 'A': (1,)}, [lam], [F(1)], [vreset], [dx], [dy], F(1, 8), Am, F(0),
                   [[b]], [[F(0)]], [[F(2)]])

def section_aux():
    print('三、原 ⊥_aux 情形与端点回归：逐例判定（信号是否经实际途径参与了有效留迹；改判与否及依据）')
    H = F(1, 2)
    cases = []
    G = gate1(F(0), F(1), H)
    cases.append(('预置资格迹例', G, ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)]), ('none', (F(1),))))
    for Am in (F(0), F(1, 16)):
        for lam, dx, dy in itertools.product((H, F(1)), repeat=3):
            Gx = gate1(lam, dx, dy, Am)
            cand = []
            if lam == 1: cand.append(('v=1/2', ([[F(1, 4)]], [H], [F(0)], [F(0)])))
            if dx == 1: cand.append(('x=1', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)])))
            if dy == 1: cand.append(('y=1', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(1)])))
            if len(cand) > 1:
                cand.append(('全部非零', ([[F(1, 4)]], [H if lam == 1 else F(0)], [F(1) if dx == 1 else F(0)], [F(1) if dy == 1 else F(0)])))
            cand.append(('零辅助态', Gx.init()))
            for nm, oo in cand:
                for sig in (('A', (F(1),)), ('none', (F(1),)), ('A', (F(0),))):
                    cases.append((f'A₋={fr(Am)} λ={fr(lam)} d_x={fr(dx)} d_y={fr(dy)} {nm}', Gx, oo, sig))
    tab = {}
    n_case = n_ev = 0
    lines = []
    for nm, Gx, oo, sig in cases:
        I = GK.Instance(nm, Gx, [sig], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=oo)
        rows = classify4(I)
        n_case += 1
        for r in rows:
            n_ev += 1
            k = (r['old'], r['cls'])
            tab[k] = tab.get(k, 0) + 1
            if r['old'] == '⊥_aux' or nm.startswith('预置资格迹例'):
                lines.append(f"    {nm}｜{sig[0]}＋I={fr(F(sig[1][0]))}｜P4R {int(r['p4r'])} P4Z {int(r['p4z'])}｜{ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])}｜严格子域 {r['old']} → PS {r['cls']}"
                             f"｜链：{r['why']}｜{show_wit(r)}｜共同参与：{'；'.join(coparticipants(I, r['e']))}")
    print(f'  共 {n_case} 例（预置资格迹例；A₋ ∈ {{0, 1/16}} × λ、d_x、d_y ∈ {{1/2, 1}} 的 8 组 × 各非零静止辅助态与零辅助态 × 字母 A＋I=1、none＋I=1、A＋I=0），有效写入 {n_ev} 个。')
    print('  原 ⊥_aux 事件逐一（严格子域判 ⊥_aux 者全列）：')
    for ln in lines:
        print(ln)
    print('  汇总（严格子域 → PS：事件数）：' + '；'.join(f'{a} → {b}：{c}' for (a, b), c in sorted(tab.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0]))))
    print('  读法：每一原 ⊥_aux 事件都有自入场分量出发的实际链与同拍容许撤除见证（M1₀），信号参与了有效留迹；预置辅助位（x、v、y 之非零者）只作为共同参与者列出，'
          '不构成负判（申报来源读法）。零辅助态上的原 S*₀ 正例同由 M1₀ 认证。无一例由 ⊥_aux 改判为 0 或保留 ⊥。')
    print()

# ---------------- 四：过定情形（逐例） ----------------
def section_od():
    print('四、过定情形：信号经实际途径参与、而单项撤除不改变写入（逐例）')
    H = F(1, 2)
    # 例 OD-1：信号间过定（两路信号各自足以越阈）；x0 由拍 0 的入口留下（跨拍辅助记忆）
    G2 = GC.Gate(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'a': (1, 0), 'b': (0, 1), 'ab': (1, 1)}, [H], [F(1)], [F(0)], [F(1), F(1)], [H, H],
                 F(1, 8), F(0), F(0), [[F(1, 4)], [F(1)]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(2)], [F(2)]])
    I1 = GK.Instance('OD-1', G2, [('a', (F(0),)), ('b', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)])
    rows = [r for r in classify4(I1) if r['e']['t'] == 1]
    e = rows[0]['e']
    singles = []
    for sig in removals(G2, I1.hist[1]):
        S = changed_comps(G2, I1.hist[1], sig, 1)
        evs2 = RW.Ev(G2, I1.pre[1], sig, 1)
        singles.append(f"撤 {'、'.join(cname(x) for x in sorted(S))}：{event_change(evs2, e) or '不变'}")
    print(f"  OD-1（信号与信号过定）：拍 1 字母 b（w10 = 1 单独越阈）＋I = 1（单独越阈），x0 = 1 由拍 0 入口留下；{ename(e)} c = {fr(e['c'])}")
    print(f"    撤除逐一：{'；'.join(singles)}")
    print(f"    判：{rows[0]['cls']}（{show_wit(rows[0])}）——单项撤除不改变，联合撤除即不发生；撤除域本含任意分量子集，故由 M1₀ 认证，已证闭合。")
    # 例 OD-2、OD-3：信号与预置辅助位（v）过定
    for tag, Iv in (('OD-2', F(1, 4)), ('OD-3', F(1))):
        Gx = gate1(F(1), F(1), H)
        I = GK.Instance(tag, Gx, [('none', (Iv,))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=([[F(1, 4)]], [F(1)], [F(1)], [F(0)]))
        r = classify4(I)[0]
        print(f"  {tag}（信号与预置膜电位过定）：λ = 1，预置 v = 1（λv = 1 = θ，单独越阈），x = 1，字母 none＋I = {fr(Iv)}；{ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])}")
        print(f"    链：{r['why']}；同拍撤除 I：门控仍开（λv ≥ θ），写入不变；无较早拍")
        print(f"    判：{r['cls']}" + (f"（{show_wit(r)}；对应前件 B_OD）" if r['cls'] == 'M2' else '（NESS 不成立：含 I 而不含 λv 的子集 {I} 不足以越阈；r 不判 0）'))
    # 例 OD-4：跨拍可认证（复位值 v_reset = θ 使门控逐拍连发）
    Gx = gate1(F(1), F(1), H, vreset=F(1))
    I = GK.Instance('OD-4', Gx, [('A', (F(1),)), ('none', (F(1, 4),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)])
    r = [x for x in classify4(I) if x['e']['t'] == 1][0]
    print(f"  OD-4（同拍过定、跨拍可认证）：λ = 1、v_reset = θ = 1；拍 0 A＋I=1 发放（v 复位为 1），拍 1 none＋I=1/4；{ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])}")
    print(f"    同拍撤除 I@1：门控仍开（λv = 1）；判：{r['cls']}（{show_wit(r)}；途径经辅助位 v、x，须 EF1′）")
    print('  读法：过定分三类——信号间过定由联合撤除（M1₀）认证；同拍由界内贡献过定而较早拍的信号经辅助位决定该界内贡献者由迹应钳定撤除（M1_aux，须 EF1′）认证；'
          '其余由 NESS（M2，须 B_OD）认证或记 ⊥_OD。⊥_OD 只表示本篇认证方法不足，不是 0。')
    print()

# ---------------- 五：引理 CH₀、CH、定理 SUB、STR 的有限核验与随机来源分类 ----------------
def section_checks():
    print('五、有限核验')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_ch = v_ch = n_bad = bad_noorig = 0
    for _ in range(150):
        G = S3.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 8)
        h = S3.random_hist(rng, G, L)
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=X)
        ch0, _, _ = chains4(I)
        for t in range(L):
            for sig in removals(G, h[t]):
                S = changed_comps(G, h[t], sig, t)
                ok = no_activation(G, I.pre[t], h[t], sig, I.kw)
                evs2 = RW.Ev(G, I.pre[t], sig, t, **I.kw)
                for e in I.evs[t].values():
                    if e['tgt'][0] != 'w' or abs(e['c']) <= EPS or event_change(evs2, e) is None:
                        continue
                    o0 = ch0[t].get((e['id'][0], e['id'][1]), set())
                    if ok:
                        n_ch += 1; v_ch += (not (S & o0))
                    else:
                        n_bad += 1; bad_noorig += (not (S & o0))
    print(f'  引理 CH₀（同拍：容许撤除改变有效写入 ⇒ 所撤分量属其 Ch^D15 起点）：150 个随机成员，容许且改变 {n_ch} 次，违例 {v_ch}；'
          f'不容许（开启门控）而改变 {n_bad} 次，其中所撤分量不属起点 {bad_noorig} 次（说明无开启条件不可省）')
    rng = random.Random(20261103)
    n4 = v4 = v3 = 0
    for _ in range(120):
        G = S3.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 7)
        h = S3.random_hist(rng, G, L)
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=X)
        _, chA, _ = chains4(I)
        _, chO, _ = S3.chains(I)
        for u in range(L - 1):
            for sig in removals(G, h[u]):
                S = changed_comps(G, h[u], sig, u)
                _, _, g2 = clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, L - 1)
                for t in range(u + 1, L):
                    if not all(all(b <= a for a, b in zip(I.gates[tau], g2[tau])) for tau in range(u, t + 1)):
                        break
                    evs2, _, _ = clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, t)
                    for e in I.evs[t].values():
                        if e['tgt'][0] != 'w' or abs(e['c']) <= EPS or GK.same_event(evs2, e):
                            continue
                        k = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
                        n4 += 1
                        v4 += not (S & chA[t].get(k, set()))
                        v3 += not (S & chO[t].get(k, set()))
    print(f'  引理 CH（跨拍：迹应钳定、全程无开启的撤除改变有效写入 ⇒ 所撤分量属其 Ch^D15 跨拍起点）：120 个随机成员，改变 {n4} 次，'
          f'按 Ch^D15 违例 {v4}；按严格子域链（LEAK 分项代 λ）违例 {v3}（严格子域链在 λ ∈ {{0, 1}} 或 v = 0 处与函数支持不符）')
    # 定理 SUB：P4R（或较弱的 P4G ∧ P5ₑ）下一切有效写入由 M1₀ 认证；定理 STR：M1₀ 失败只在 RELAX、门控过定的 PLUS（s_i = 0）与成员都不可认证的 CLIP
    rng = random.Random(7)
    tot = {}
    sub_n = sub_v = g_n = g_v = str_v = 0
    old_new = {}
    for _ in range(120):
        G = S3.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 7)
        h = S3.random_hist(rng, G, L)
        T = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=T)
        rows = classify4(I, lookback=3, tmax=L - 1)
        for r in rows:
            tot[r['cls']] = tot.get(r['cls'], 0) + 1
            k = (r['old'], r['cls']); old_new[k] = old_new.get(k, 0) + 1
            e = r['e']; t = e['t']
            if r['p4r']:
                sub_n += 1; sub_v += (r['cls'] != 'M1₀')
            nl = null_letter(G)
            p4g = not any(gates_at(G, I.pre[t], nl, I.kw))
            p5e = event_change(RW.Ev(G, I.pre[t], nl, t, **I.kw), e) is not None
            if p4g and p5e and h[t] != nl:
                g_n += 1; g_v += (r['cls'] != 'M1₀')
            if r['cls'] != 'M1₀':
                kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
                okk = (kind == 'RELAX') or (kind == 'PLUS' and not G.pat[h[t][0]][idx[0]] and gate_overdetermined(I, e)) or \
                      (kind == 'CLIP')
                str_v += (not okk)
    print(f'  定理 SUB：拍前态满足 P4R 的有效写入 {sub_n} 个，非 M1₀ {sub_v}；满足 P4G ∧ P5ₑ（静置下无门控开启、e 在静置下改变）者 {g_n} 个，非 M1₀ {g_v}')
    print(f'  定理 STR（lo ≥ 0）：M1₀ 不成立的有效写入全属 RELAX、s_i = 0 且门控过定的 PLUS、或 CLIP：违例 {str_v}')
    print('  随机来源分类（120 个随机成员，T = 全部突触；种子同严格子域）：' + '；'.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(tot.items())))
    print('  严格子域 → PS（事件数）：' + '；'.join(f'{a} → {b}：{c}' for (a, b), c in sorted(old_new.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0]))))
    # 过定族：λ = 1、v_reset ∈ {0, 1, 3/2}、预置辅助态随机（含越阈的 v），专门产生门控过定的写入，检验 M1_aux、M2、⊥_OD 与定理 STR
    rng = random.Random(20261104)
    tot2, str2, m2chk, auxchk = {}, 0, 0, 0
    n2 = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        n_in, n_out = 2, 1
        pat = {'none': (0, 0), 'a': (1, 0), 'b': (0, 1), 'ab': (1, 1)}
        vr = rng.choice([F(0), F(1), F(3, 2)])
        G = GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, [F(1)], [F(1)], [vr], [rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)]) for _ in range(n_in)], [F(1, 2)],
                    F(1, 8), F(rng.randint(0, 1), 16), F(0), [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8)] for _ in range(n_in)],
                    [[F(0)] for _ in range(n_in)], [[F(2)] for _ in range(n_in)])
        o0 = ([[G.b[i][0]] for i in range(n_in)], [F(rng.randint(0, 6), 4)], [F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for _ in range(n_in)], [F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2)])
        L = rng.randint(2, 5)
        h = [(rng.choice(list(pat)), (F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2, 4]), 4),)) for _ in range(L)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 0, T=[('w', i, 0) for i in range(n_in)], o0=o0)
        rows = classify4(I, lookback=4, old=False)
        for r in rows:
            n2 += 1
            tot2[r['cls']] = tot2.get(r['cls'], 0) + 1
            e = r['e']; t = e['t']
            if r['cls'] == 'M2':
                tag, c, A, N = r['wit']
                sA = sum((a for (tg, a) in gate_summands(G, I.pre[t], h[t], e['id'][1][1]) if tg in A), F(0))
                m2chk += not (sA + N < G.theta[0] <= sA + N + c and not m1_0(I, e)[0])
            if r['cls'] == 'M1_aux':
                u, sig, S, _ch = r['wit']
                auxchk += not (S & r['oa'])
            if r['cls'] not in ('M1₀',):
                kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
                okk = (kind == 'RELAX') or (kind == 'PLUS' and not G.pat[h[t][0]][idx[0]] and gate_overdetermined(I, e)) or \
                      (kind == 'CLIP' and not any(m1_0(I, m)[0] for m in I.evs[t].values()
                                                  if m['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') and m['id'][1] == idx and abs(m['c']) > EPS))
                str2 += (not okk)
    print(f'  过定族（200 个成员：λ = 1，v_reset ∈ {{0, 1, 3/2}}，预置辅助态随机）：有效写入 {n2} 个，分类 ' + '；'.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(tot2.items())) +
          f'；M2 见证复核失败 {m2chk}；M1_aux 见证所撤分量不属跨拍起点 {auxchk}；定理 STR 违例 {str2}')
    print()

# ---------------- 六：D16 的来源忠实条件 ----------------
def part_mark(M, hist):
    """F 模型上的参与型来源 Mark^A：条款 0（界外载体或界外脉冲的有效改变，即信号本身作用于结构位）；
    条款 1：载体在 B 内、发生由字母门控的机制（静置下不运行），把本拍字母撤为静置即不发生，且撤除不开启任何实际未运行的机制（无开启）。
    F 中 B 内机制只读结构位的拍前值（D12），同拍撤除不改其分项；跨拍依赖只经结构位（已留迹应），不计。"""
    ev_all, _ = M.run(hist)
    mark = {i: [] for i in M.T}
    for t, u, s, evs in ev_all:
        names = {e['name'] for e in evs}
        h2 = list(hist); h2[t] = 'rest'
        ev2 = M.run(h2[:t + 1])[0][t][3]
        n2 = {e['name'] for e in ev2}
        noact = n2 <= names
        for e in evs:
            if e['tgt'] not in M.T or abs(e['c']) <= M.eps:
                continue
            if e['car'] == '界外':
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], '条款0'))
            elif u != 'rest' and noact and e['name'] not in n2:
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], '条款1'))
    return mark

def section_d16():
~~~
